This parameter-compute mismatch (Section 1.2 ) is especially acute for offloading because activation memory does not decrease with Expert Parallelism (EP) or Pipeline Parallelism (PP); these strategies reduce parameter memory, not activation memory.

细粒度 MoE 模型的参数膨胀极为严重：DeepSeek-V3 每个 token 仅激活 37B（18 $\times$ 的比例，表 3）；Kimi-K2 总参数达 1T，激活 32B（31 $\times$）。这种参数-计算失配（第 1.2 节）对卸载尤为严重，因为激活值内存不会随专家并行或流水线并行而减少；这些策略减少的是参数内存，而非激活值内存。

Transformer Engine provides layer-level offloading, but this coarse granularity limits effectiveness. Different modules within a layer have significantly different memory-to-compute ratios: LayerNorm activations are small and cheap to recompute, while expert_fc1 inputs are large but computationally expensive. Layer-level offloading cannot distinguish these cases. It either offloads everything (wasting bandwidth) or nothing (wasting memory).

Transformer Engine 提供层级卸载，但这种粗粒度限制了效果。层内不同模块的内存-计算比差异显著：LayerNorm 激活值小、重计算便宜，而 expert_fc1 输入大、计算昂贵。层级卸载无法区分这些情况：要么全部卸载（浪费带宽），要么全不卸载（浪费内存）。

The GPU’s Copy Engine and Compute Engine operate independently. When a module’s computation time exceeds its activation transfer time, the D2H copy can run concurrently with subsequent computation at zero cost. Figure 9 illustrates the stream overlap mechanism for both forward and backward passes.

GPU 的拷贝引擎与计算引擎独立运行。当一个模块的计算时间超过其激活值传输时间时，D2H 拷贝可以与后续计算并发运行，零成本。图 9 展示了前向与反向传播的流重叠机制。

图： Figure 9 : Fine-grained activation offloading: stream overlap for forward and backward passes.

图 9：细粒度激活值卸载：前向与反向传播的流重叠。

During forward propagation, input activations are offloaded to CPU immediately after module computation, running in parallel with the next module’s computation via a dedicated D2H stream. One exception: the last layer’s activations are not offloaded because they are needed immediately during backward, so no computation is available to hide the transfer latency.

在前向传播期间，输入激活值在模块计算后立即卸载到 CPU，通过专用的 D2H 流与下一个模块的计算并行运行。有一个例外：最后一层的激活值不卸载，因为它们在反向传播期间立即需要，没有可用于隐藏传输延迟的计算。

During backward propagation, activation reloading follows a Layer-Staggered Reload pattern: the system reloads the activation of the same module (e.g., expert_fc1 ) from the next layer while computing gradients for the current layer. The reload occurs after backward completes for each module, so only one activation per module type resides in GPU memory at any time, avoiding the need to double activation storage. This is essential when a single module has very large activations where 2 $\times$ memory footprint would cause unexpected memory peaks.

在反向传播期间，激活值重载遵循逐层交错重载（Layer-Staggered Reload）模式：系统在计算当前层的梯度时，从下一层重载同一模块（例如 expert_fc1）的激活值。重载在每个模块的反向传播完成后发生，因此任何时刻每种模块类型只有一个激活值驻留在 GPU 内存中，避免了激活值存储翻倍。当单个模块的激活值非常大、2 $\times$ 的内存占用会导致意外的内存峰值时，这一点至关重要。

For PP/VPP scenarios, a ChunkOffloadHandler manages the offloading/reloading logic for each (microbatch, VPP stage) combination, identical to the PP=1 case. The main challenge is managing data dependencies and execution order between virtual pipeline chunks. Handlers are enqueued into a deque with VPP stages in reverse order (FILO) and microbatches in normal order (FIFO). During backward, the popped handler automatically matches the VPP chunk execution order, correctly pairing offloaded activations with their corresponding backward passes.

对于 PP/VPP 场景，ChunkOffloadHandler 管理每个（微批次（micro-batch），VPP 阶段）组合的卸载/重载逻辑，与 PP=1 的情况相同。主要挑战是管理虚拟流水线（virtual pipeline）块之间的数据依赖与执行顺序。处理程序按 VPP 阶段倒序（FILO）与微批次正序（FIFO）入队到双端队列中。在反向传播期间，弹出的处理程序自动匹配 VPP 块的执行顺序，正确地把卸载的激活值与其对应的反向传播配对。

Module-level granularity: Users specify which modules to offload via --offload-modules , enabling mixed strategies. Lightweight modules (LayerNorm, activations) use recomputation while expensive ones (attention, experts) use offloading.

模块级粒度：用户通过 --offload-modules 指定要卸载哪些模块，从而实现混合策略。轻量模块（LayerNorm、激活函数）使用重计算，而昂贵的模块（注意力、专家）使用卸载。

Asynchronous transfers: Dedicated D2H/H2D CUDA streams run transfers in parallel with computation; CUDA events coordinate synchronization only when necessary.

异步传输：专用的 D2H/H2D CUDA 流使传输与计算并行运行；CUDA 事件仅在必要时协调同步。

Recomputation integration: Combines with fine-grained recomputation (section 4.1.4 ). For moe_act , both strategies apply: recompute the activation while offloading its input, releasing the entire fc1 $\rightarrow$ act chain.

重计算集成：与细粒度重计算（第 4.1.4 节）结合。对于 moe_act，两种策略都适用：重新计算激活函数，同时卸载其输入，释放整个 fc1 $\rightarrow$ act 链。

CUDA Graphs compatibility: Uses external events rather than stream synchronization, allowing offloading modules to be hidden by computations outside the CUDA graph.

CUDA 图兼容性：使用外部事件而非流同步，使卸载模块可以被 CUDA 图之外的计算隐藏。

Full-scenario compatibility: Supports PP=1/PP $>$ 1/VPP $>$ 1, all precisions (BF16/FP8/MXFP8/NVFP4), 1F1B with all-to-all overlap, and MoE/MLA architectures.

全场景兼容性：支持 PP=1/PP $>$ 1/VPP $>$ 1、所有精度（BF16/FP8/MXFP8/NVFP4）、带全互联重叠的 1F1B（单前向单后向）以及 MoE/MLA 架构。

Full recomputation stores each layer’s input on GPU while releasing intermediate activations; backward recomputes intermediates from these stored inputs. For an $L$ -layer model, GPU peak memory is $L\times\text{layer_input}+1\times\text{layer_intermediate}$ . In contrast, offloading moves layer inputs to CPU; during backward, each input is reloaded just before use and released immediately after. This reduces GPU peak memory up to $1\times\text{layer_input}+1\times\text{layer_intermediate}$ , independent of model depth. For deep models (e.g., 60+ layers), this represents a fundamental memory advantage that full recomputation cannot achieve.

全重计算把每层的输入存储在 GPU 上，同时释放中间激活值；反向传播从这些存储的输入重新计算中间结果。对于 $L$ 层模型，GPU 峰值内存为 $L\times\text{layer_input}+1\times\text{layer_intermediate}$。相比之下，卸载把层输入移到 CPU；在反向传播期间，每个输入在使用前即时重载，使用后立即释放。这把 GPU 峰值内存降低到 $1\times\text{layer_input}+1\times\text{layer_intermediate}$，与模型深度无关。对于深层模型（例如 60 层以上），这代表了全重计算无法实现的根本性内存优势。

Fine-grained offloading and recomputation work together as complementary strategies (figure 10 ): lightweight operations like LayerNorm use recomputation, while expensive modules like attention and experts use offloading. Asynchronous transfers overlap with computation to hide PCIe latency. Table 5 shows results across multiple configurations. Fine-grained offloading reduces memory by 10–18% with only 1.6–2% throughput overhead. In the case of training Qwen3-235B, offloading enables reducing Tensor Parallelism degree, which improves throughput by 15.0% with nearly the same memory cost.

细粒度卸载与重计算作为互补策略协同工作（图 10）：LayerNorm 等轻量操作使用重计算，注意力与专家等昂贵模块使用卸载。异步传输与计算重叠以隐藏 PCIe 延迟。表 5 展示了多个配置下的结果。细粒度卸载以仅 1.6–2% 的吞吐（throughput）开销减少 10–18% 的内存。以训练 Qwen3-235B 为例，卸载使张量并行（tensor parallelism，TP）度数得以降低，吞吐提高 15.0%，内存成本几乎相同。

表： Table 5 : Memory and throughput impact of fine-grained activation offloading.

表 5：细粒度激活值卸载对内存与吞吐的影响。

模型与配置 基线 +卸载 内存 $\Delta$ 吞吐 $\Delta$
DeepSeek-V3 完整 169 GB 151 GB $-$ 10.7% $-$ 1.6%
TP1PP8EP32VPP4, MXFP8 945 TF/s 930 TF/s
Qwen3-235B 172 GB 175 GB $+$ 1.7% $+$ 15.0%
TP2 $\rightarrow$ TP1 + EP16 $\rightarrow$ EP64 800 TF/s 920 TF/s

图： Figure 10 : Fine-grained offloading and recomputation: complementary memory optimization strategies.

图 10：细粒度卸载与重计算：互补的内存优化策略。

4.1.6. Weight and Optimizer Optimization: Low-Precision Storage and Offloading（4.1.6. 权重与优化器优化：低精度存储与卸载）

The preceding optimizations target activation memory, which dominates the memory breakdown. However, Table 3 shows that main weights and optimizer states consume 32.1 GB per GPU, representing 16% of the total memory footprint. For models with hundreds of billions of parameters, this component becomes a significant optimization target. Megatron-Core provides two techniques: precision-aware optimization that reduces storage requirements, and CPU offloading that moves inactive state off-GPU.

前面的优化针对激活值内存，它在内存分解中占主导。然而，表 3 显示主权重（main weights）与优化器状态每 GPU 消耗 32.1 GB，占总内存占用的 16%。对于拥有数千亿参数的模型，这一部分成为重要的优化目标。Megatron-Core 提供两种技术：降低存储需求的精度感知优化，以及把非活跃状态移出 GPU 的 CPU 卸载。

The Adam optimizer [ 56 ] maintains two state tensors per parameter: first moment ( exp_avg ) and second moment ( exp_avg_sq ). Traditional implementations store these in FP32, consuming 8 bytes per parameter, which poses a significant memory bottleneck for large-scale training. The key insight is that optimizer states can tolerate lower storage precision without affecting convergence, provided that the actual update computation remains in higher precision.

Adam 优化器 [ 56 ] 为每个参数维护两个状态张量：一阶动量（exp_avg）与二阶动量（exp_avg_sq）。传统实现以 FP32 存储它们，每个参数消耗 8 字节，这对大规模训练构成显著的内存瓶颈。关键洞见是，优化器状态可以容忍更低的存储精度而不影响收敛，前提是实际的更新计算保持较高精度。

The precision-aware optimizer decouples storage precision from computation precision [ 57 ] . The first and second moments can be stored in BF16 (2 bytes each) or even FP8 (1 byte each), reducing per-parameter storage from 8 bytes to 4 bytes (BF16) or 2 bytes (FP8). During each optimizer step, these lower-precision states are dynamically cast to FP32 within TransformerEngine’s FusedAdam kernel; the update is computed with full-precision arithmetic to maintain numerical stability.

精度感知优化器将存储精度与计算精度解耦 [ 57 ] 。一阶与二阶动量可以以 BF16（每个 2 字节）甚至 FP8（每个 1 字节）存储，把每个参数的存储从 8 字节降至 4 字节（BF16）或 2 字节（FP8）。在每次优化器步进时，这些低精度状态在 TransformerEngine 的 FusedAdam 内核内动态转换为 FP32，以全精度算术计算更新，从而保持数值稳定性。

The implementation provides four configurable precision levels: main gradients, main parameters, first moment, and second moment precision. In typical configurations, main parameters and gradients remain in FP32 to ensure high-quality gradient updates, while moment estimates are stored in BF16 [ 58 ] . This achieves approximately 50% reduction in optimizer state memory (roughly 10–12 GB savings from the 32.1 GB budget in Table 3 ) with negligible impact on training dynamics.

该实现提供四个可配置的精度级别：主梯度、主参数、一阶动量与二阶动量精度。在典型配置中，主参数与主梯度保持 FP32，以确保高质量的梯度更新，而动量估计以 BF16 存储 [ 58 ] 。这使优化器状态内存减少约 50%（从表 3 中 32.1 GB 的预算中节省约 10–12 GB），对训练动态的影响可忽略不计。

When combined with the distributed optimizer, which shards optimizer states across data-parallel ranks of size $d$ , the theoretical memory requirement per rank is further reduced. In DeepSeek-V3 training with BF16 moments, the memory consumption per parameter per DP rank decreases from $6+12/d$ bytes to $6+8/d$ bytes.

与分布式优化器结合使用时——它把优化器状态分片到大小为 $d$ 的数据并行 rank 上——每个 rank 的理论内存需求进一步降低。在使用 BF16 动量的 DeepSeek-V3 训练中，每个数据并行 rank 每个参数的内存消耗从 $6+12/d$ 字节降至 $6+8/d$ 字节。

During forward and backward passes, optimizer states occupy GPU memory but remain inactive; offloading reclaims this memory for other operations. Optimizer state offloading keeps the optimizer step on GPU but transfers optimizer states ( exp_avg , exp_avg_sq ) and master weights to CPU after optimizer.step() and reloads them before the next step. This approach uses GPU compute while reclaiming memory during forward and backward passes.

在前向与反向传播期间，优化器状态占用 GPU 内存但保持非活跃状态；卸载可回收这部分内存供其他操作使用。优化器状态卸载让优化器步进保留在 GPU 上，但把优化器状态（exp_avg、exp_avg_sq）与主权重在 optimizer.step() 之后传输到 CPU，并在下一步之前重新加载。这种方法使用 GPU 计算，同时在前向与反向传播期间回收内存。

State offloading is particularly effective on systems with high-bandwidth interconnects. On GB200 with NVLink-C2C, asynchronous transfers overlap with computation, and pinned memory enables maximum bandwidth utilization. For DeepSeek-V3, state offloading saves 15–20 GB of GPU memory (47–62% of the 32.1 GB optimizer and weight budget) with only 0.1–0.2 seconds per iteration overhead.

状态卸载在具有高带宽互连的系统上尤其有效。在配备 NVLink-C2C 的 GB200 上，异步传输与计算重叠，固定内存（pinned memory）实现最大带宽利用率。对于 DeepSeek-V3，状态卸载可节省 15–20 GB GPU 内存（32.1 GB 优化器与权重预算的 47–62%），每次迭代仅增加 0.1–0.2 秒开销。

These two techniques offer trade-offs that work well together. The precision-aware optimizer incurs no performance overhead since the FP32 cast occurs within the fused Adam kernel; it reduces optimizer state memory by up to 50%. CPU offloading saves more memory (all the optimizer state and master weights) but introduces modest transfer overhead. Importantly, the techniques compose well: using precision-aware storage with BF16 moments reduces the state size that must be offloaded, thereby decreasing transfer time and making offloading more practical even on systems without the highest-bandwidth interconnects.

这两种技术提供了可以很好协同的折中。精度感知优化器没有性能开销，因为 FP32 转换发生在融合的 Adam 内核内；它把优化器状态内存减少多达 50%。CPU 卸载节省的内存更多（全部优化器状态与主权重），但引入了适度的传输开销。重要的是，这两种技术组合得很好：使用 BF16 动量的精度感知存储减小了必须卸载的状态大小，从而缩短传输时间，即使在没有最高带宽互连的系统上，卸载也更实用。

4.1.7. FSDP for MoE（4.1.7. 面向 MoE 的 FSDP）

Fully Sharded Data Parallelism (FSDP) [ 53 , 33 ] shards model parameters, gradients, and optimizer states across data-parallel ranks so that each GPU holds only a local shard. Expert parameters often dominate memory in MoE models, making FSDP a natural complement to Expert Parallelism. However, the two must compose correctly.

完全分片数据并行（FSDP）[ 53 , 33 ] 把模型参数、梯度与优化器状态分片到数据并行 rank 上，使每个 GPU 只持有局部分片。专家参数在 MoE 模型中常常主导内存，使完全分片数据并行成为专家并行的天然补充。然而，两者必须正确组合。

Megatron-FSDP composes seamlessly with multiple parallel strategies, including Expert Parallelism (EP), Tensor Parallelism (TP), and Context Parallelism (CP). For large-scale MoE models, combining FSDP with EP turns the general advantages of FSDP into concrete benefits for expert-heavy workloads.

Megatron-FSDP 与多种并行策略无缝组合，包括专家并行、张量并行（tensor parallelism，TP）与上下文并行（context parallelism，CP）。对于大规模 MoE 模型，把完全分片数据并行与专家并行结合起来，可将完全分片数据并行的一般优势转化为针对专家密集型工作负载的具体收益。

Reduced Memory and Communication : EP assigns each GPU a subset of experts, and FSDP shards those local experts across the expert data-parallel (EDP) group rather than the full DP group. Per-GPU memory and collective volume both scale with EDP size instead of total DP size, so MoE models can support more experts or larger batches under the same hardware budget.

内存与通信减少：专家并行为每个 GPU 分配一部分专家，而完全分片数据并行把这些本地专家分片到专家数据并行（expert data parallelism，EDP）组上，而非整个数据并行组。每 GPU 内存与集合通信量都随专家数据并行规模而非总数据并行规模扩展，因此 MoE 模型可以在相同的硬件预算下支持更多专家或更大的批量。

PP-free Flexibility : FSDP+EP avoids several engineering pain points of pipeline parallelism, including uneven PP/VPP stage balancing, placement of output and MTP layers in DeepSeek-style models, and partitioning vision encoders in multimodal settings. Configuration reduces to choosing EP size and FSDP sharding degree, without complex pipeline-stage design.

无需流水线并行的灵活性：完全分片数据并行与专家并行的组合避免了流水线并行的几个工程痛点，包括 PP/VPP 阶段平衡不均匀、DeepSeek 风格模型中输出层与多 token 预测（Multi-Token Prediction，MTP）层的放置，以及多模态设置中视觉编码器的划分。配置简化为选择专家并行规模与完全分片数据并行分片度，无需复杂的流水线阶段设计。

Broad Model Compatibility : Megatron-FSDP supports two model implementation paths: models built with Megatron-Core’s own modules and PyTorch-native models composed from standard torch.nn modules. Both paths receive the same FSDP+EP sharding, communication optimizations, and checkpointing support. For interoperability with the HuggingFace ecosystem, Megatron-Bridge handles online weight conversion between HuggingFace models and Megatron FSDP, so users can load a pretrained HuggingFace checkpoint, train with FSDP+EP, and export weights back without manual format conversion.

广泛的模型兼容性：Megatron-FSDP 支持两种模型实现路径：使用 Megatron-Core 自身模块构建的模型，以及由标准 torch.nn 模块组成的 PyTorch 原生模型。两种路径获得相同的完全分片数据并行与专家并行分片、通信优化与检查点支持。为了与 HuggingFace 生态互操作，Megatron-Bridge 在 HuggingFace 模型与 Megatron FSDP 之间在线转换权重，用户可以加载预训练的 HuggingFace 检查点，用完全分片数据并行与专家并行训练，再导出权重，无需手动格式转换。

The core design challenge is that dense and expert layers need different sharding scopes. Dense modules (attention, normalization) benefit from FSDP sharding over the full DP group. EP partitions expert modules first, so each GPU holds only its assigned experts. FSDP should therefore shard within each expert’s data-parallel (EDP) group, not globally.

核心设计挑战在于，稠密层与专家层需要不同的分片范围。稠密模块（注意力、归一化）受益于完全分片数据并行在整个数据并行组上的分片。专家并行先对专家模块分区，因此每个 GPU 只持有其分配的专家。因此，完全分片数据并行应在每个专家数据并行组内分片，而非全局分片。

Megatron-FSDP resolves this through a dual DeviceMesh architecture. A primary DeviceMesh governs DP-Shard, DP-Outer, TP, and CP for dense modules. An auxiliary Expert DeviceMesh manages EP modules with FSDP scoped to the EDP dimension. Each transformer layer routes its sub-modules to the appropriate mesh automatically. Attention and normalization use the primary mesh, while MoE expert FFN layers use the Expert DeviceMesh. As a result, AllGather and ReduceScatter for expert parameters stay within small EDP groups instead of spanning all DP ranks.

Megatron-FSDP 通过双 DeviceMesh 架构解决这一问题。主 DeviceMesh 管理稠密模块的 DP-Shard、DP-Outer、TP 与 CP。辅助的专家 DeviceMesh 以完全分片数据并行管理专家并行模块，完全分片数据并行的作用范围限定在专家数据并行维度。每个 Transformer 层自动把子模块路由到合适的网格。注意力与归一化使用主网格，而 MoE 专家 FFN 层使用专家 DeviceMesh。因此，专家参数的全收集（all-gather）与归约散射（reduce-scatter）保留在较小的专家数据并行组内，而非跨越所有数据并行 rank。

For large-scale deployments, this design extends to Hybrid Sharded Data Parallelism (HSDP), which adds an outer DP replication dimension. HSDP fully shards parameters within a subset of ranks and replicates across subsets, bounding AllGather to the intra-group size. The dual DeviceMesh keeps separate outer-DP groups for expert and non-expert parameters, exploiting the bandwidth gap between NVLink (intra-node) and the scale-out interconnect (inter-node).

对于大规模部署，这一设计扩展到混合分片数据并行（Hybrid Sharded Data Parallelism，HSDP），它增加了一个外层数据并行复制维度。混合分片数据并行在一部分 rank 内完全分片参数，并在子集之间复制，把全收集限定在组内规模。双 DeviceMesh 为专家参数与非专家参数保留各自的外层数据并行组，利用 NVLink（节点内）与横向扩展互连（节点间）之间的带宽差距。

The dual DeviceMesh determines which ranks participate in each FSDP collective, but collectives themselves still carry overhead from buffer management and data copying. Megatron-FSDP eliminates this overhead through two optimizations.

双 DeviceMesh 决定了哪些 rank 参与每个完全分片数据并行集合通信，但集合通信本身仍有缓冲区管理与数据拷贝带来的开销。Megatron-FSDP 通过两项优化消除了这一开销。

  1. Non-uniform sharding: Standard FSDP2 shards each parameter independently along its primary dimension, producing uniform per-parameter shards (Figure 11(a) ). Megatron-FSDP instead flattens and concatenates all parameters within a module, then applies non-uniform sharding across devices (Figure 11(b) ). Shard boundaries then align with communication buffer layouts, and collectives read directly from flattened storage without redundant copying. In Llama3 405B training, this reduces communication overhead by roughly 10%.
  1. 非均匀分片：标准 FSDP2 沿每个参数的主维度独立分片，产生均匀的逐参数分片（图 11(a)）。Megatron-FSDP 则把一个模块内的所有参数展平并拼接，然后跨设备应用非均匀分片（图 11(b)）。分片边界于是与通信缓冲区布局对齐，集合通信直接从展平存储中读取，无需冗余拷贝。在 Llama3 405B 训练中，这使通信开销减少约 10%。

图： (a) FSDP2 per-parameter uniform sharding.

（a）FSDP2 逐参数均匀分片。

图： (a) FSDP2 per-parameter uniform sharding.

（a）FSDP2 逐参数均匀分片。

图： (b) Megatron-FSDP per-module non-uniform sharding.

（b）Megatron-FSDP 逐模块非均匀分片。

  1. Persistent double buffers with NCCL User Buffer Registration: Baseline FSDP frequently allocates and frees communication buffers, and NCCL copies data between user buffers and its internal staging area on every collective. Megatron-FSDP pre-allocates two persistent buffers at training start and cycles between them (Figure 12 ), eliminating allocation churn. Megatron-FSDP then registers these buffers with NCCL via User Buffer Registration (UBR), so NCCL reads and writes the pre-registered memory directly without intermediate copies. The combined effect is true zero-copy communication. On NVLink systems, the SM footprint of communication kernels drops from 8–32 SMs to 1–4 SMs. On SHARP-enabled InfiniBand, network switches handle reductions and free GPU SMs entirely.
  1. 持久双缓冲与 NCCL 用户缓冲区注册：基线 FSDP 频繁分配与释放通信缓冲区，而 NCCL 在每次集合通信时都会在用户缓冲区与其内部暂存区之间拷贝数据。Megatron-FSDP 在训练开始时预分配两个持久缓冲区并循环使用（图 12），消除了分配抖动。Megatron-FSDP 随后通过用户缓冲区注册（User Buffer Registration，UBR）向 NCCL 注册这些缓冲区，使 NCCL 直接读写预注册的内存，无需中间拷贝。综合效果是真正的零拷贝通信。在 NVLink 系统上，通信内核的 SM 占用从 8–32 个 SM 降至 1–4 个 SM。在支持 SHARP 的 InfiniBand 上，网络交换机处理归约，完全释放 GPU SM。

图： Figure 12 : Persistent double-buffer design: two pre-allocated buffers are cycled across FSDP collectives, eliminating allocation overhead and enabling NCCL User Buffer Registration.

图 12：持久双缓冲设计：两个预分配的缓冲区在 FSDP 集合通信间循环使用，消除分配开销并支持 NCCL 用户缓冲区注册。

Even with zero-copy collectives, AllGather and ReduceScatter still occupy the network while GPUs wait for parameters or gradient synchronization. Megatron-FSDP pipelines these collectives with computation using dedicated CUDA streams: AllGather for the next FSDP unit is issued while the current unit’s forward or backward pass is still executing, and ReduceScatter for gradients runs concurrently with the backward pass of subsequent layers. Increasing the micro-batch size extends the computation window available for hiding communication, though at the cost of higher memory usage. Users can tune this trade-off through the overlap_param_gather and overlap_grad_reduce flags.

即使有了零拷贝集合通信，全收集与归约散射在 GPU 等待参数或梯度同步时仍会占用网络。Megatron-FSDP 使用专用 CUDA 流把这些集合通信与计算流水线化：下一个 FSDP 单元的全收集在当前单元的前向或反向传播仍在执行时发出，梯度的归约散射与后续层的反向传播并发运行。增大微批次大小可以延长可用于隐藏通信的计算窗口，但代价是更高的内存使用。用户可以通过 overlap_param_gather 与 overlap_grad_reduce 标志调节这一折中。

4.1.8. Summary（4.1.8. 小结）

Table 6 summarizes the memory optimization techniques described in this section and their primary targets.

表 6 总结了本节介绍的内存优化技术及其主要目标。

表： Table 6 : Summary of memory optimization techniques.

表 6：内存优化技术总结。

技术 内存目标 代价
降低精度训练 激活值 数值精度与 CPU 开销
内存高效置换 激活值 -
细粒度重计算 激活值 计算开销
细粒度卸载 激活值 CPU 开销与未重叠拷贝
精度感知优化器 优化器状态 数值精度
FSDP（配合 EP） 参数 + 优化器 通信开销

These memory optimizations complement each other: Memory-Efficient Permutation eliminates redundant storage with zero overhead; FP8/FP4 activations reduce precision with minimal quality impact; fine-grained recomputation offers favorable compute-memory trade-offs for specific modules; activation offloading provides additional headroom when other techniques are insufficient; low-precision storage and state offloading reduce weight and optimizer memory; and FSDP enables scaling beyond single-device capacity. Together, they reduce memory from a blocking barrier to a manageable constraint.

这些内存优化相互补充：内存高效置换以零开销消除冗余存储；FP8/FP4 激活值以最小的质量影响降低精度；细粒度重计算为特定模块提供有利的计算-内存折中；激活值卸载在其他技术不足时提供额外空间；低精度存储与状态卸载减少权重与优化器内存；完全分片数据并行使扩展超出单设备容量成为可能。它们共同把内存从一个阻碍性障碍变为可管理的约束。

Memory optimization is not a one-time gate that, once passed, can be forgotten. For large-scale MoE models, memory is a persistently scarce resource throughout the entire optimization lifecycle. Beyond enabling training to proceed at all, memory headroom unlocks other optimizations: larger batch sizes provide more computation to hide communication latency (Section 4.2.3 ), CUDA Graphs require additional static buffers (Section 4.3.6 ), and EP communication overlap must hold activations from multiple microbatches simultaneously. Many optimizations described in the following sections consume memory, and the techniques above are what make that consumption feasible.

内存优化不是一次性的关卡，通过后就可以忘掉。对于大规模 MoE 模型，内存是在整个优化生命周期中持续稀缺的资源。除了让训练得以进行，内存余量还解锁了其他优化：更大的批量提供更多计算来隐藏通信延迟（第 4.2.3 节），CUDA 图需要额外的静态缓冲区（第 4.3.6 节），专家并行通信重叠必须同时持有多个微批次的激活值。后续几节介绍的许多优化都会消耗内存，而上述技术正是使这种消耗成为可能的原因。

4.2. Breaking the Communication Wall（4.2. 打破通信墙）

Communication overhead directly reduces GPU utilization: every microsecond spent in collective operations represents lost compute capacity. Before optimization, EP all-to-all communication typically consumes 20–60% of training time, depending on model configuration, EP size, and hardware topology. When EP stays within the NVLink domain (e.g., DeepSeek-V3 with EP64 on GB200 NVL72), the overhead is around 20%; when EP crosses node boundaries (e.g., DeepSeek-V3 with EP64 on H100 across nodes), it rises to 40–60%. The techniques in this section target all points on this spectrum.

通信开销直接降低 GPU 利用率：花在集合通信操作上的每一微秒都代表着损失的计算能力。优化前，专家并行（EP）全互联（all-to-all）通信通常占用训练时间的 20–60%，具体取决于模型配置、专家并行规模和硬件拓扑。当专家并行保留在 NVLink 域内时（例如 DeepSeek-V3 在 GB200 NVL72 上使用 EP64），开销约为 20%；当专家并行跨越节点边界时（例如 DeepSeek-V3 在 H100 上跨节点使用 EP64），开销上升到 40–60%。本节的技术针对这一范围内的所有情况。

Expert Parallelism (EP) distributes experts across devices to scale MoE models beyond single-device capacity, but this distribution introduces a unique communication pattern. Unlike AllReduce, whose volume is independent of parallelism degree, all-to-all volume is proportional to token count and hidden dimension, and larger EP pushes this communication cross-node where bandwidth is limited. For fine-grained MoE architectures like DeepSeek-V3 and Kimi-K2, three factors compound this challenge:

专家并行（EP）将专家（expert）分布到多个设备上，使混合专家（MoE）模型的规模超越单设备的容量，但这种分布带来了一种独特的通信模式。与 AllReduce 不同——其通信量与并行度无关——全互联的通信量与 token 数量和隐藏维度成正比，而更大的专家并行会把这种通信推向跨节点，那里的带宽有限。对于 DeepSeek-V3 和 Kimi-K2 这类细粒度混合专家架构，以下三个因素加剧了这一挑战：

High frequency : More experts mean more dispatch/combine operations per layer (DeepSeek-V3 has 58 MoE layers, each requiring two all-to-all operations).

高频：专家越多，每层所需的分发（dispatch）/合并（combine）操作就越多（DeepSeek-V3 有 58 个混合专家层，每层需要两次全互联操作）。

Cross-node bottleneck : With large EP sizes spanning multiple nodes, inter-node all-to-all latency dominates due to lower bandwidth.

跨节点瓶颈：当大规模专家并行横跨多个节点时，节点间全互联的延迟因带宽较低而占主导。

Low arithmetic intensity : Small experts complete computation quickly, leaving less time to overlap with communication.

低算术强度：小专家很快完成计算，留给与通信重叠的时间更少。

4.2.1. Communication Anatomy: The Expert Parallel Pattern（4.2.1. 通信剖析：专家并行模式）

图： Figure 13 : Expert parallelism across 4 GPUs with 4 experts.

图 13：4 块 GPU、4 个专家的专家并行。

Figure 13 illustrates the expert parallelism communication pattern. In standard EP implementations, each MoE layer requires two collective communication operations: dispatch sends tokens to their assigned experts, and combine returns processed tokens to their original ranks. For a model with $L$ MoE layers, $B$ tokens per batch, hidden dimension $h$ , and EP degree EP , each forward pass involves $2L$ dispatch/combine operations, each transferring $O(Bh)$ data across EP ranks.

图 13 展示了专家并行通信模式。在标准的专家并行实现中，每个混合专家层需要两次集合通信操作：分发将 token 发送到其指定的专家，合并将处理后的 token 返回到其原始 rank。对于一个有 $L$ 个混合专家层、每批次 $B$ 个 token、隐藏维度 $h$、专家并行度为 EP 的模型，每次前向传播涉及 $2L$ 次分发/合并操作，每次操作在专家并行 rank 之间传输 $O(Bh)$ 的数据。

At DeepSeek-V3 scale, this translates to 58 MoE layers $\times$ 2 operations/layer = 116 dispatch/combine operations per forward pass. The backward pass doubles this count. At 50 GB/s inter-node bandwidth (e.g., InfiniBand NDR), a single dispatch with 200 MB payload takes several milliseconds, accumulating to hundreds or thousands of milliseconds per iteration.

在 DeepSeek-V3 的规模下，这相当于 58 个混合专家层 $\times$ 2 次操作/层 = 每次前向传播 116 次分发/合并操作。反向传播使这一数量翻倍。在 50 GB/s 的节点间带宽下（例如 InfiniBand NDR），一次携带 200 MB 有效载荷的分发需要数毫秒，累积起来每次迭代达到数百甚至数千毫秒。

Communication volume is determined by the EP configuration and cannot be reduced without changing the parallelism strategy. Optimization must therefore target how communication is executed and scheduled. Two strategies work together to address communication overhead:

通信量由专家并行配置决定，不改变并行策略就无法减少。因此，优化必须针对通信的执行和调度方式。以下两种策略共同应对通信开销：

Maximize bandwidth utilization. Standard NCCL all-to-all implementations do not fully exploit available bandwidth, particularly for fine-grained MoE workloads. Optimized dispatchers (DeepEP, HybridEP) use specialized kernels that fuse operations and exploit hardware primitives to approach peak bandwidth.

最大化带宽利用率。标准的 NCCL 全互联实现没有充分利用可用带宽，特别是对于细粒度混合专家工作负载。优化的分发器（DeepEP、混合专家并行（HybridEP））使用专用内核融合操作并利用硬件原语，以接近峰值带宽。

Hide latency behind computation. Even with optimal bandwidth, all-to-all operations take time. By overlapping communication with computation from adjacent microbatches, this latency can be hidden rather than exposed on the critical path.

将延迟隐藏在计算之后。即使带宽达到最优，全互联操作仍然需要时间。通过将通信与相邻微批次（micro-batch）的计算重叠，这一延迟可以被隐藏起来，而不是暴露在关键路径上。

The following subsections present techniques organized by these strategies: bandwidth optimization (DeepEP, HybridEP), and latency hiding (EP communication overlapping, pipeline integration).

以下小节按这些策略组织介绍相关技术：带宽优化（DeepEP、混合专家并行）与延迟隐藏（专家并行通信重叠、流水线集成）。

4.2.2. DeepEP and HybridEP: Maximizing EP Bandwidth（4.2.2. DeepEP 与混合专家并行：最大化专家并行带宽）

In conventional EP implementations, token exchange relies on all-to-all communication. Even with optimized NCCL collectives, this path has inherent limitations: a permutation stage is needed before dispatch, which replicates each token top- $k$ times and creates redundant traffic. In some settings, this preprocessing also surfaces as host overhead.

在传统的专家并行实现中，token 交换依赖全互联通信。即使使用优化的 NCCL 集合通信，这一路径也存在固有局限：分发前需要一个置换阶段，它将每个 token 复制 top- $k$ 次，产生冗余流量。在某些场景下，这种预处理还会带来主机开销。

To address these issues, Megatron-Core provides two token-based dispatch backends, HybridEP and DeepEP [ 23 ] . Token-based dispatch eliminates the permutation step and avoids sending redundant tokens, reducing overall communication volume and improving effective bandwidth.

为解决这些问题，Megatron-Core 提供了两个基于 token 的分发后端：混合专家并行和 DeepEP [ 23 ]。基于 token 的分发消除了置换步骤，避免发送冗余 token，从而减少总通信量并提高有效带宽。

HybridEP was developed by NVIDIA. It follows the same token-based principle as DeepEP, exploits hardware primitives such as TMA and IBGDA, and targets comparable or higher bandwidth with lower SM usage, including Multi-Node NVLink (MNNVL) deployments.

混合专家并行由 NVIDIA 开发。它遵循与 DeepEP 相同的基于 token 的原理，利用 TMA 和 IBGDA 等硬件原语，以更低的 SM 使用量实现相当或更高的带宽，包括多节点 NVLink（MNNVL）部署。

图： Figure 14 : The dispatch kernel design of HybridEP.

图 14：混合专家并行的分发内核设计。

For dispatch, as illustrated in figure 14 , HybridEP reads data from global memory into shared memory based on routing information, then writes tokens to destinations through a FIFO queue. In the inter-node case, instead of sending duplicated payloads directly through the network interface, HybridEP first uses an RDMA warp group to exchange data between GPUs with the same local index across nodes, then forwards within each node. This reduces cross-node traffic and allows inter-node and intra-node transfers to overlap.

对于分发，如图 14 所示，混合专家并行根据路由信息将数据从全局内存读入共享内存，然后通过 FIFO 队列将 token 写入目标位置。在节点间场景下，混合专家并行不是通过网络接口直接发送重复的有效载荷，而是先使用 RDMA warp 组在跨节点具有相同本地索引的 GPU 之间交换数据，然后再在每个节点内转发。这减少了跨节点流量，并使节点间传输与节点内传输能够重叠。

图： Figure 15 : The combine kernel design of HybridEP.

图 15：混合专家并行的合并内核设计。

For combine, standard all-to-all dispatch performs communication only, so a separate unpermutation stage is required afterward. HybridEP instead fuses reduction into the communication kernel. As shown in figure 15 , HybridEP reads data through a FIFO queue, performs reduction, and writes results directly to target locations. In inter-node settings, HybridEP first performs reduction across nodes for data that must be communicated cross-node, then completes a second reduction within each node.

对于合并，标准的全互联分发只执行通信，因此之后还需要一个单独的反置换阶段。混合专家并行则将归约融合到通信内核中。如图 15 所示，混合专家并行通过 FIFO 队列读取数据、执行归约，并将结果直接写入目标位置。在节点间场景下，混合专家并行先对必须跨节点通信的数据执行跨节点归约，然后在每个节点内完成第二次归约。

We evaluate all-to-all dispatch and HybridEP on GB200 and H100 with hidden size 7168, sequence length 4096, and 256 experts under multiple EP sizes. Results are summarized in Table 7 . Across all tested settings, HybridEP consistently outperforms all-to-all, with larger gains in inter-node scenarios. The table reports communication latency only; in end-to-end training, the gap is typically larger once permutation and host overhead are included.

我们在 GB200 和 H100 上评估了全互联分发与混合专家并行，隐藏维度为 7168、序列长度为 4096、专家数量为 256，并在多种专家并行规模下进行了测试。结果汇总于表 7。在所有测试配置下，混合专家并行始终优于全互联，在节点间场景下的优势更大。该表仅报告通信延迟；在端到端训练中，一旦计入置换和主机开销，差距通常会更大。

表： Table 7 : EP Scaling Performance for HybridEP and all-to-all (in µs).

表 7：混合专家并行与全互联的专家并行扩展性能（单位：微秒）。

操作 专家并行规模 GB200 混合专家并行 (µs) GB200 全互联 (µs) H100 混合专家并行 (µs) H100 全互联 (µs)
分发 8 391 735 661 1265
分发 16 578 743 1485 5774
分发 32 612 769 3064 8059
分发 64 675 930 4626 9164
合并 8 353 741 624 1277
合并 16 527 765 1688 5628
合并 32 646 758 3088 7815
合并 64 744 827 4398 8727

4.2.3. EP Communication Overlapping: Hiding EP Communication Latency（4.2.3. 专家并行通信重叠：隐藏专家并行通信延迟）

Optimized dispatchers improve all-to-all bandwidth utilization , but the fundamental issue remains: all-to-all still lies on the end-to-end critical path. For DeepSeek-V3 with EP64, EP all-to-all communication can still account for 30–40% of iteration time, directly limiting throughput.

优化的分发器提高了全互联的带宽利用率，但根本问题依然存在：全互联仍然位于端到端关键路径上。对于使用 EP64 的 DeepSeek-V3，专家并行全互联通信仍可占迭代时间的 30–40%，直接限制了吞吐（throughput）。

The key observation is that all-to-all latency can be hidden behind computation when enough independent work exists in the overlap window [ 59 , 60 ] . For fine-grained MoE models such as DeepSeek-V3 and Kimi-K2, this is challenging because cross-node EP communication can consume about half of layer time, while adjacent operators (for example, GEMMs) are often too short to hide it.

关键观察在于，当重叠窗口内存在足够多的独立工作时，全互联延迟可以隐藏在计算之后 [ 59 , 60 ]。对于 DeepSeek-V3 和 Kimi-K2 这类细粒度混合专家模型，这很有挑战性，因为跨节点专家并行通信可能占用约一半的层时间，而相邻算子（例如 GEMM）往往太短，无法将其隐藏。

To address this imbalance, Megatron-Core uses a dedicated 1F1B all-to-all overlap scheme [ 61 ] that merges forward and backward passes from neighboring micro-batches and interleaves compute and all-to-all kernels across CUDA streams [ 39 , 38 ] . Concretely, backward all-to-all for one micro-batch overlaps with forward attention/MLP for another. Conceptually, this is a DualPipe-like [ 62 ] bidirectional schedule built on top of standard 1F1B while preserving Megatron-Core compatibility.

为解决这种不平衡，Megatron-Core 采用一种专用的单前向单后向（1F1B）全互联重叠方案 [ 61 ]，该方案合并相邻微批次的前向与反向传播，并在 CUDA 流之间交错计算内核与全互联内核 [ 39 , 38 ]。具体而言，一个微批次的反向全互联与另一个微批次的前向注意力/MLP 重叠。从概念上讲，这是一种类 DualPipe [ 62 ] 的双向调度，建立在标准单前向单后向之上，同时保持与 Megatron-Core 的兼容性。

To enable all-to-all overlap in 1F1B, we merge adjacent micro-batch forward and backward passes and evaluate two patterns:

为了在单前向单后向中实现全互联重叠，我们合并相邻微批次的前向与反向传播，并评估两种模式：

Merged FWD-FWD / BWD-BWD : This strategy merges passes of the same type from two micro-batches (figure 16 ). In FWD-FWD , forward passes for micro-batches 0 and 1 run in parallel and overlap all-to-all with computation. BWD-BWD applies the same principle to backward passes. This approach has clear trade-offs:

合并的前向-前向（FWD-FWD）/反向-反向（BWD-BWD）：该策略合并两个微批次中相同类型的前向或反向传播（图 16）。在前向-前向中，微批次 0 和 1 的前向传播并行运行，并将全互联与计算重叠。反向-反向对反向传播采用相同的原理。这种方法有明确的权衡：

Memory Overhead : 2x peak activation memory.

内存开销：2 倍峰值激活值（activation）内存。

all-to-all Overlap : Less opportunity to hide all-to-all because forward computation is roughly half of backward computation.

全互联重叠：隐藏全互联的机会更少，因为前向计算大约只有反向计算的一半。

图： Figure 16 : Merged FWD-FWD Timeline with all-to-all Overlapping.

图 16：带全互联重叠的合并前向-前向时间线。

Merged FWD-BWD (DualPipe Equivalent) : This is the preferred strategy (figure 17 ). It merges the forward pass of one micro-batch with the backward pass of another (for example, FWD of micro-batch 1 with BWD of micro-batch 0). Compared with FWD-FWD , it offers:

合并的前向-反向（FWD-BWD，DualPipe 等价方案）：这是首选策略（图 17）。它将一个微批次的前向传播与另一个微批次的反向传播合并（例如，微批次 1 的前向传播与微批次 0 的反向传播）。与前向-前向相比，它具有以下优势：

Memory Overhead : No additional memory overhead (activations from the forward pass are reused for the backward pass).

内存开销：没有额外的内存开销（前向传播的激活值被反向传播复用）。

Compatibility : Matches DualPipe’s design but avoids complex scheduling.

兼容性：与 DualPipe 的设计一致，但避免了复杂的调度。

Limitation : The first FWD and last BWD remain on the end-to-end critical path, so their all-to-all cannot be hidden.

局限性：第一个前向传播和最后一个反向传播仍在端到端关键路径上，因此它们的全互联无法被隐藏。

图： Figure 17 : Merged FWD-BWD Timeline with all-to-all Overlapping.

图 17：带全互联重叠的合并前向-反向时间线。

To maximize all-to-all hiding in merged FWD-BWD, we use two key optimizations:

为了在合并的前向-反向中最大化全互联的隐藏效果，我们采用两项关键优化：

Stream Separation : We split workloads into two CUDA streams:

流分离：我们将工作负载拆分到两个 CUDA 流中：

Compute Stream : Runs forward/backward computations (e.g., attention, expert MLP).

计算流：运行前向/反向计算（例如，注意力、专家 MLP）。

Comm Stream : Runs all-to-all communication (e.g., token dispatch/combine for EP).

通信流：运行全互联通信（例如，专家并行的 token 分发/合并）。

By alternating tasks across streams, all-to-all runs in parallel with computation—minimizing idle cycles.

通过在两个流之间交替执行任务，全互联与计算并行运行，从而最大限度地减少空闲周期。

W/D Split (Weight-Gradient / Data-Gradient Split) [ 6 ] : A key dependency blocks overlap: backward dispatch (B/dispatch) requires outputs from backward MLP (B/mlp). To break this dependency, we split backward MLP into:

W/D 拆分（权重梯度/数据梯度拆分）[ 6 ]：一个关键的依赖关系阻碍了重叠：反向分发（B/dispatch）需要反向 MLP（B/mlp）的输出。为了打破这一依赖关系，我们将反向 MLP 拆分为：

W/mlp : Weight gradient calculation (independent of B/dispatch).

W/mlp：权重梯度计算（与 B/dispatch 无关）。

D/mlp : Data gradient calculation (feeds into B/dispatch).

D/mlp：数据梯度计算（输入到 B/dispatch）。

This split reduces compute-stream idle time: W/mlp can overlap with F/mlp to hide B/dispatch when F/mlp alone is too short.

这种拆分减少了计算流的空闲时间：当 F/mlp 本身太短时，W/mlp 可以与 F/mlp 重叠，以隐藏 B/dispatch。

Figure 18 summarizes these optimizations. The baseline (top) executes sequentially, where all-to-all blocks computation. The 1F1B FWD-BWD scheme (bottom) interleaves adjacent micro-batches so communication can be hidden behind compute. The W/D split further increases overlap opportunities. Together, these methods reduce EP communication overhead from 30–40% (after DeepEP) to under 5% of iteration time in DeepSeek-V3 training on H100.

图 18 总结了这些优化。基线（顶部）顺序执行，其中全互联阻塞了计算。单前向单后向的前向-反向方案（底部）交错执行相邻微批次，使通信可以隐藏在计算之后。W/D 拆分进一步增加了重叠机会。这些方法共同将 DeepSeek-V3 在 H100 上训练时的专家并行通信开销从 30–40%（使用 DeepEP 后）降低到迭代时间的 5% 以下。

图： Figure 18 : EP all-to-all communication overlap strategies: baseline vs. 1F1B with W/D split.

图 18：专家并行全互联通信重叠策略：基线与带 W/D 拆分的单前向单后向方案。

To scale overlap to large models, merged FWD-BWD can be combined with Interleaved Pipeline Parallelism (Interleaved PP), which partitions the model into virtual pipeline stages (VPP) and expands overlap opportunities across pipeline ranks. Figure 19 shows an interleaved 1F1B schedule with three phases: warmup, 1F1B, and flush. Adjacent FWD-BWD pairs in the 1F1B phase can apply the EP overlap pattern above. However, adjacent pairs may still have data dependencies when they belong to the same micro-batch. To avoid this, we run one extra micro-batch at the end of warmup before entering 1F1B, ensuring adjacent FWD/BWD pairs are dependency-free and enabling full all-to-all overlap across virtual stages.

为了将重叠扩展到大模型，合并的前向-反向可以与交错流水线并行（Interleaved PP）结合，后者将模型划分为虚拟流水线阶段（VPP），并在流水线 rank 之间扩展重叠机会。图 19 展示了一个包含三个阶段的交错单前向单后向调度：预热、单前向单后向与排空。单前向单后向阶段中相邻的前向-反向对可以应用上述专家并行重叠模式。然而，当相邻的对属于同一个微批次时，它们之间仍可能存在数据依赖。为避免这一点，我们在预热结束时、进入单前向单后向之前多运行一个微批次，确保相邻的前向/反向对没有依赖关系，从而在虚拟阶段之间实现完全的全互联重叠。

A latency comparison between merged FWD-BWD (1F1B) and DualPipeV (a refined DualPipe variant) shows:

合并的前向-反向（单前向单后向）与 DualPipeV（DualPipe 的改进变体）之间的延迟对比表明：

1F1B is faster with large VPP sizes (more virtual stages enable more overlapping opportunities).

在 VPP 规模较大时，单前向单后向更快（更多的虚拟阶段带来更多的重叠机会）。

The latency gap shrinks at large PP sizes and micro-batch counts (both strategies reach near-optimal overlap).

在流水线并行规模和微批次数量较大时，延迟差距缩小（两种策略都接近最优重叠）。

For hybrid models (e.g., DeepSeek-V3 with mixed dense/MoE layers), workload balance across PP stages is critical. Megatron-Core’s Flexible Asymmetric VPP supports custom per-stage layer placement to maximize overlap.

对于混合模型（例如 DeepSeek-V3 的稠密层/混合专家层混合结构），流水线并行阶段之间的工作负载均衡至关重要。Megatron-Core 的灵活非对称 VPP（Flexible Asymmetric VPP）支持自定义每阶段的层放置，以最大化重叠。

图： Figure 19 : Interleaved PP Timeline with all-to-all Overlapping.

图 19：带全互联重叠的交错流水线并行时间线。

Several factors limit the achievable speedup from all-to-all overlap:

有几个因素限制了全互联重叠可实现的加速比：

Proportion of overlapped batches : More micro-batches increase the proportion of overlapped batches.

重叠批次的比例：微批次越多，重叠批次的比例就越高。

all-to-all Proportion : EP overlap gains are larger when all-to-all dominates, especially for cross-node EP and fine-grained MoE models with low compute-to-communication ratios.

全互联占比：当全互联占主导时，专家并行重叠的收益更大，特别是对于跨节点专家并行和计算通信比较低的细粒度混合专家模型。

SM Carve-out Overhead : Reserving SMs for all-to-all can reduce GEMM efficiency. In our DeepSeek-V3 benchmark, DeepEP uses 20 SMs per GPU and introduces roughly 20% GEMM-efficiency overhead.

SM 预留开销：为全互联预留 SM 会降低 GEMM 效率。在我们的 DeepSeek-V3 基准测试中，DeepEP 每块 GPU 使用 20 个 SM，并带来约 20% 的 GEMM 效率开销。

On fine-grained MoE models such as DeepSeek-V3, combining all-to-all overlap with optimized dispatchers (Section 4.2.2 ) achieves a 93% overlap ratio for expert communication latency, reducing expert communication share from 30–40% of iteration time to under 5%.

在 DeepSeek-V3 这类细粒度混合专家模型上，将全互联重叠与优化的分发器（第 4.2.2 节）结合，可使专家通信延迟的重叠率达到 93%，将专家通信在迭代时间中的占比从 30–40% 降低到 5% 以下。

4.2.4. Summary（4.2.4. 总结）

The communication wall optimizations work together: DeepEP/HybridEP maximize bandwidth utilization; FWD-BWD overlap hides latency behind computation; Interleaved PP extends overlap opportunities across pipeline stages; Flexible VPP enables optimal load balance. Combined, they reduce all-to-all’s contribution to training time under 10%.

通信墙的各项优化协同工作：DeepEP/混合专家并行最大化带宽利用率；前向-反向重叠将延迟隐藏在计算之后；交错流水线并行将重叠机会扩展到流水线阶段之间；灵活 VPP 实现最优负载均衡（load balancing）。综合起来，它们将全互联对训练时间的贡献降低到 10% 以下。

With communication latency hidden behind computation, high GPU utilization might be expected. However, profiling reveals the Compute Efficiency Wall, which has two distinct aspects: kernel efficiency (fine-grained experts produce small GEMMs that underutilize GPU resources) and host overhead (numerous small operations create gaps between kernel executions where the GPU awaits work from the host).

随着通信延迟隐藏在计算之后，人们可能会预期 GPU 利用率会很高。然而，性能分析揭示了计算效率墙，它有两个不同的方面：内核效率（细粒度专家产生的小 GEMM 没有充分利用 GPU 资源）和主机开销（大量小操作在内核执行之间造成空隙，此时 GPU 在等待主机分配工作）。

4.3. Breaking the Compute Efficiency Wall（打破计算效率墙）

Compute efficiency is the final constraint: even with sufficient memory and hidden communication, GPU resources can remain underutilized if kernels are inefficient or the host cannot dispatch work fast enough. As model and expert counts grow, compute inefficiencies compound, making optimization essential for achieving peak hardware utilization.

计算效率是最后一道约束：即便内存充足、通信被隐藏，如果内核效率低下或主机分发工作的速度不够快，GPU 资源仍可能得不到充分利用。随着模型规模与专家（expert）数量增长，计算低效会不断累积，因此优化对于实现峰值硬件利用率至关重要。

4.3.1. Compute Anatomy: Sources of Inefficiency（计算剖析：低效的来源）

Compute inefficiency in MoE training stems from two distinct sources:

混合专家（Mixture of Experts，MoE）训练中的计算低效源于两个不同的来源：

Kernel Efficiency. Fine-grained MoE architectures produce workloads that underutilize GPU resources. DeepSeek-V3’s 256 small experts yield GEMMs with M dimensions of $\sim$ 128 tokens per expert, far from the thousands needed for peak Tensor Core utilization. Beyond expert computation, MoE introduces complex routing and dispatching operations composed of many small kernels that individually cannot saturate GPU compute capacity.

内核效率。细粒度混合专家架构产生的工作负载无法充分利用 GPU 资源。DeepSeek-V3 的 256 个小专家产生的 GEMM，每个专家的 M 维约为 $\sim$ 128 个 token，远低于 Tensor Core 达到峰值利用率所需的数千规模。除专家计算外，混合专家还引入了复杂的路由与分发（dispatch）操作，这些操作由许多小内核组成，单个内核都无法使 GPU 的计算能力饱和。

Host Overhead. Numerous small operations create kernel launch overhead, and the CPU cannot dispatch work fast enough to keep the GPU saturated. Three factors make this worse:

主机开销。大量小操作带来内核启动开销，CPU 分发工作的速度不够快，无法让 GPU 保持饱和。以下三个因素使情况更糟：

Fine-grained experts : Many individual GEMMs rather than few large ones, each requiring a separate kernel launch.

细粒度专家：许多独立的小 GEMM，而非少数大 GEMM，每个都需要单独启动一次内核。

Reduced-precision training : Additional quantization kernels add to the launch overhead.

低精度训练：额外的量化内核增加了启动开销。

Dropless routing : Dynamic token counts require host-device synchronization to determine tensor shapes, placing the CPU on the critical path.

无丢弃（dropless）路由：动态变化的 token 数量需要通过主机-设备同步来确定张量形状，使 CPU 处于关键路径上。

The result is host-boundedness : GPU kernels separated by microseconds of host-side latency, visible as gaps in profiling traces where compute resources sit idle.

结果是主机受限（host-boundedness）：GPU 内核之间隔着微秒级的主机端延迟，在性能分析轨迹中表现为计算资源闲置的空隙。

Kernel shapes are determined by model architecture and cannot be changed without modifying the model. Optimization must therefore target how computation is organized and scheduled. Five strategies work together to address compute inefficiency:

内核形状由模型架构决定，不修改模型就无法改变。因此优化必须针对计算的组织与调度方式。以下五种策略共同作用，以解决计算低效问题：

Fuse related operations. Kernel fusion consolidates routing, permutation, and auxiliary loss computations into fewer, larger kernels.

融合相关操作。内核融合（kernel fusion）将路由、置换与辅助损失（auxiliary loss）计算合并为更少、更大的内核。

Accelerate with low precisions. Reduced-precision training in FP8/FP4 uses faster Tensor Core operations, increasing throughput for the same kernel shapes.

用低精度加速。FP8/FP4 低精度训练使用更快的 Tensor Core 操作，在相同的内核形状下提高吞吐（throughput）。

Eliminate per-iteration CPU logic. CUDA Graphs capture kernel sequences and replay them with minimal host involvement.

消除每轮迭代的 CPU 逻辑。CUDA 图（CUDA Graphs）捕获内核序列，并以极少的主机参与重放它们。

Remove host-device synchronization. Sync-free execution enables GPU kernels to proceed without waiting for shape information from the host.

消除主机-设备同步。免同步执行使 GPU 内核无需等待主机提供的形状信息即可继续执行。

Balance expert load. ECHO dynamically clones hot experts to underutilized ranks, reducing load imbalance that causes some ranks to wait while others compute.

平衡专家负载。ECHO（动态专家克隆）将热门专家动态克隆到未充分利用的 rank 上，减轻负载不均衡——负载不均衡会导致一些 rank 在其他 rank 计算时空等。

The following subsections present techniques organized by these strategies: batching and fusion for kernel efficiency (Section 4.3.2 ), low-precision acceleration (Section 4.3.5 ), CUDA Graphs for host overhead elimination (Section 4.3.6 ), sync-free execution for dropless MoE (Section 4.3.7 ), and load balancing via ECHO (Section 4.3.7 ).

以下各小节按这些策略介绍相关技术：为提升内核效率而做的批处理与融合（第 4.3.2 节），低精度加速（第 4.3.5 节），用 CUDA 图消除主机开销（第 4.3.6 节），面向无丢弃混合专家（dropless MoE）的免同步执行（第 4.3.7 节），以及通过 ECHO 实现的负载均衡（load balancing）（第 4.3.7 节）。

4.3.2. Grouped GEMM and Kernel Fusion: Improving Kernel Efficiency（分组 GEMM 与内核融合：提升内核效率）

The most direct approach to improving kernel efficiency is combining small operations into larger ones. Grouped GEMM batches expert computations for better hardware utilization [ 24 ] , while kernel fusion consolidates routing and permutation operations into fewer kernels [ 63 ] . Megatron-Core implements these optimizations at three levels: expert computation (Grouped GEMM), token routing (Permutation Fusion), and router logic (Router and Aux-Loss Fusion).

提升内核效率最直接的方法是将小操作合并为大操作。分组 GEMM（Grouped GEMM）将专家计算打包成批，以提高硬件利用率 [ 24 ] ，而内核融合将路由与置换操作合并为更少的内核 [ 63 ] 。Megatron-Core 在三个层面实现这些优化：专家计算（分组 GEMM）、token 路由（置换融合，Permutation Fusion）与路由器逻辑（路由器与辅助损失融合，Router and Aux-Loss Fusion）。

The computation of experts is essentially a series of independent GEMMs. Grouped GEMM improves performance over separate sequential GEMMs by overlapping the wave tail effect of kernels.

专家的计算本质上是一系列独立的 GEMM。分组 GEMM 通过重叠内核的波尾效应（wave tail effect），比逐个执行的独立 GEMM 性能更好。

Megatron-Core provides two Grouped GEMM implementations:

Megatron-Core 提供两种分组 GEMM 实现：

Multi-stream launch of cuBLASLt GEMMs. By launching individual cuBLASLt GEMMs into multiple CUDA streams, they can overlap with each other. This method supports various precision and scaling modes, including BF16, per-tensor FP8, blockwise FP8, MXFP8, and NVFP4, with performance comparable to highly optimized Grouped GEMM implementations.

cuBLASLt GEMM 的多流启动。将单个 cuBLASLt GEMM 启动到多个 CUDA 流中，它们可以相互重叠。该方法支持多种精度与缩放模式，包括 BF16、逐张量 FP8、块级 FP8、MXFP8 与 NVFP4，性能与高度优化的分组 GEMM 实现相当。

CUTLASS Grouped GEMM. By fusing individual GEMMs into a single kernel, CUTLASS Grouped GEMM achieves better performance when the number of GEMMs is large. However, different precisions, scaling modes, and hardware platforms require individual development effort, and additional tuning is needed for different problem sizes. The current implementation in TE only supports BF16 on the Hopper platform.

CUTLASS 分组 GEMM。通过将单个 GEMM 融合为一个内核，CUTLASS 分组 GEMM 在 GEMM 数量较大时性能更优。但不同的精度、缩放模式与硬件平台都需要单独的开发工作，且针对不同的问题规模还需要额外的调优。TE 中的当前实现仅支持 Hopper 平台上的 BF16。

Two additional implementations are under development:

另外两种实现正在开发中：

cuBLASLt Grouped GEMM via CUBLASLT_BATCH_MODE_GROUPED . The cuBLASLt Grouped GEMM assumes the shape information on device and conducts the computation in one single kernel, which unblocks applying CUDA Graphs to the expert part. It covers all precisions and scaling modes with built-in heuristics, and is intended to supersede (i) and (ii) where supported.

基于 CUBLASLT_BATCH_MODE_GROUPED 的 cuBLASLt 分组 GEMM。cuBLASLt 分组 GEMM 假定形状信息位于设备端，并在单个内核中完成计算，从而使专家部分能够应用 CUDA 图。它以内置启发式方法覆盖全部精度与缩放模式，并计划在支持的地方取代 (i) 和 (ii)。

cuteDSL Grouped GEMM with fusions. Grouped GEMM fused with activation functions, quantization (for the next layer), and scaling factor swizzling via cuteDSL. These kernels are optimized for MXFP8 and NVFP4 [ 64 ] on the Blackwell platform, targeting the fprop GEMM of FC1 and dgrad GEMM of FC2. By consolidating expert computation, activation, and quantization into a single kernel, this approach significantly reduces kernel count and is compatible with CUDA Graphs.

带融合的 cuteDSL 分组 GEMM。通过 cuteDSL 将分组 GEMM 与激活（activation）函数、量化（为下一层准备）以及缩放因子重排（swizzling）融合在一起。这些内核针对 Blackwell 平台上的 MXFP8 与 NVFP4 [ 64 ] 进行了优化，目标是 FC1 的前向传播（fprop）GEMM 与 FC2 的反向梯度（dgrad）GEMM。通过将专家计算、激活与量化合并到单个内核中，该方法显著减少了内核数量，并与 CUDA 图兼容。

4.3.3. Permutation Fusion（置换融合）

Currently, Grouped GEMM requires tokens assigned to the same expert to be stored contiguously (i.e., permuted tokens). If all-to-all collective communication is enabled, further permutation is necessary to ensure tokens assigned to each rank are grouped together. Implementing efficient permutation using native PyTorch is challenging, as it tends to launch many small kernels and incurs additional CPU overhead.

目前，分组 GEMM 要求分配给同一专家的 token 连续存储（即经过置换的 token）。如果启用了全互联（all-to-all）集合通信，还需要进一步置换，以确保分配给每个 rank 的 token 聚在一起。用原生 PyTorch 实现高效置换颇具挑战，因为它往往会启动许多小内核并带来额外的 CPU 开销。

The permute fusion pipeline in the training workload (figure 20 ) consists of three stages:

训练工作负载中的置换融合流水线（图 20）包括三个阶段：

图： Figure 20 : The pipeline for permute fusion in the training process.

图 20：训练过程中置换融合的流水线。

Preprocessing: Permutation is fundamentally a data transfer process that requires tokens to be stored consecutively in the buffer corresponding to each expert. The purpose of the preprocessing step is to generate an offset map (Row ID map in figure 20 ), which indicates the offset of each token in the input and output buffers. This ensures that permute and unpermute operations can be executed efficiently. The preprocessing kernel is called only once before permute during the forward pass, while other components reuse the results.

预处理：置换本质上是一个数据传输过程，要求 token 在每个专家对应的缓冲区中连续存储。预处理步骤的目的是生成偏移映射（图 20 中的 Row ID 映射），它标明每个 token 在输入与输出缓冲区中的偏移。这确保了置换与反置换操作能够高效执行。预处理内核在前向传播中仅在置换之前调用一次，其他组件复用其结果。

Permute: The functionality of the permute kernel is straightforward: it moves tokens from the input buffer to the output buffer according to the offset map, and then passes them to the subsequent expert MLP. In memory-efficient permute, probabilities also need to be permuted, with the results directly entering the activation part of the expert.

置换：置换内核的功能很直接：它根据偏移映射将 token 从输入缓冲区移动到输出缓冲区，然后传递给随后的专家 MLP。在内存高效置换中，概率也需要置换，其结果直接进入专家的激活值部分。

Unpermute: The unpermute step is the inverse of the permute operation. Since a token will be copied multiple times and sent to different destinations during permutation, these tokens must be summed together during the combine phase. In memory-efficient permute, the combine step simply adds them up; otherwise, the probabilities are used as weights. All accumulation is performed in FP32 precision.

反置换：反置换步骤是置换操作的逆操作。由于一个 token 在置换过程中会被复制多次并发送到不同目的地，这些 token 必须在合并（combine）阶段加和在一起。在内存高效置换中，合并步骤直接将它们相加；否则，概率将用作权重。所有累加均以 FP32 精度执行。

4.3.4. Router and Aux-Loss Fusion（路由器与辅助损失融合）

The router and auxiliary loss [ 4 , 65 , 14 , 15 ] computations are other areas that generate many small kernels and introduce considerable CPU overhead. As device computing power grows, fusion becomes increasingly important.

路由器（router）与辅助损失 [ 4 , 65 , 14 , 15 ] 计算是另外两个产生大量小内核并带来可观 CPU 开销的环节。随着设备算力增长，融合变得越来越重要。

The challenge is that the router contains components difficult to fuse, such as GEMM and communication. We decompose the remaining operations into three sections, each fused into a single kernel (figure 21 ).

挑战在于路由器包含难以融合的组件，例如 GEMM 与通信。我们将其余操作分解为三个部分，每个部分融合为一个内核（图 21）。

图： Figure 21 : The workflow of the router fusion.

图 21：路由器融合的工作流程。

Score computation, including top- $k$ and softmax/sigmoid: This is the most complex function, with various branches for different models. It includes different top- $k$ algorithms (naive top- $k$ , group top- $k$ [ 17 , 6 ] ), score functions (sigmoid, softmax), their combinations, and possible scaling operations.

分数计算，包括 top- $k$ 与 softmax/sigmoid：这是最复杂的功能，针对不同模型有多种分支。它包括不同的 top- $k$ 算法（朴素 top- $k$、分组 top- $k$ [ 17 , 6 ]）、分数函数（sigmoid、softmax）、它们的组合以及可能的缩放操作。

Score computation for auxiliary loss: This is a subset of the first section. After the score function and naive top- $k$ computation, the input for auxiliary loss calculation is generated.

面向辅助损失的分数计算：这是第一部分的一个子集。在分数函数与朴素 top- $k$ 计算之后，生成用于辅助损失计算的输入。

Computation of MoE auxiliary loss: Building on step 2, the auxiliary loss computation is fused into a single kernel.

混合专家辅助损失的计算：在第 2 步的基础上，辅助损失计算被融合为单个内核。

After enabling router fusion, the workflow within the router module is shown in figure 21 . Future work may incorporate communication into the fusion scope.

启用路由器融合后，路由器模块内的工作流程如图 21 所示。未来的工作可能将通信纳入融合范围。

4.3.5. Reduced-Precision Training: Low-Precision Acceleration（低精度训练：低精度加速）

Fine-grained MoE workloads with small expert GEMMs are often memory-bandwidth bound, limiting Tensor Core utilization. Reduced-precision training addresses this issue by reducing data movement and using faster FP8/FP4 Tensor Core operations on Hopper and Blackwell GPUs.

具有小专家 GEMM 的细粒度混合专家工作负载常常受内存带宽限制，从而限制了 Tensor Core 的利用率。低精度训练通过减少数据移动并在 Hopper 与 Blackwell GPU 上使用更快的 FP8/FP4 Tensor Core 操作来解决这一问题。

From a computation efficiency perspective, reduced-precision training provides a key benefit: FP8/FP4 GEMMs maximize Tensor Core utilization by performing Linear Layer computation in FP8/FP4. Together with the memory benefits in Sections 4.1.3 , FP8 training achieves approximately 10% to 25% end-to-end performance improvement for large-scale MoE training on different hardware platforms, while FP4 gives it even more speedup.

从计算效率的角度看，低精度训练带来一个关键好处：FP8/FP4 GEMM 通过以 FP8/FP4 执行线性层计算，最大限度地提高了 Tensor Core 利用率。结合第 4.1.3 节中的内存收益，FP8 训练在大规模混合专家训练中、在不同硬件平台上实现了约 10% 至 25% 的端到端性能提升，而 FP4 带来的加速则更为显著。

For complete coverage of reduced-precision training recipes, MoE-specific challenges, and quantization strategies, see Section 5 .

关于低精度训练配方、混合专家特有的挑战与量化策略的完整内容，见第 5 节。

4.3.6. CUDA Graphs: Eliminating Host Overhead（4.3.6. CUDA 图：消除主机开销）

Kernel fusion reduces the number of kernel launches; CUDA Graphs eliminate the per-iteration cost of those launches. This subsection presents how CUDA Graphs address CPU overhead, the different strategies for drop-and-pad versus dropless MoE, and the memory optimizations required to make CUDA Graphs practical.

内核融合（kernel fusion）减少了内核启动的次数；CUDA 图消除了这些启动在每次迭代中的开销。本小节介绍 CUDA 图如何解决 CPU 开销、丢弃-填充（drop-and-pad）与无丢弃 MoE 两种情形下的不同策略，以及使 CUDA 图切实可用的内存优化。

In MoE training, CPU overhead often becomes the dominant performance bottleneck. This overhead stems from three sources:

在 MoE 训练中，CPU 开销常常成为主导性的性能瓶颈。这种开销来自三个方面：

Python execution : Interpreter overhead, C foreign function interface (CFFI), and garbage collection introduce latency.

Python 执行：解释器开销、C 外部函数接口（CFFI）与垃圾回收都会引入延迟。

Framework overhead : Even simple operations such as torch.empty add microseconds of host-side cost; library layers (TransformerEngine, cuBLAS, cuDNN) add more.

框架开销：即使是 torch.empty 这样简单的操作也会增加数微秒的主机端开销；库层面（TransformerEngine、cuBLAS、cuDNN）还会增加更多开销。

Kernel launch overhead : Each kernel launch incurs several microseconds of CPU-side cost.

内核启动开销：每次内核启动都会产生数微秒的 CPU 端开销。

Three trends amplify this in modern training:

在现代训练中，三大趋势加剧了这一问题：

Faster GPUs : As kernel execution speeds increase, less time remains to overlap CPU work, making CPU overhead increasingly visible.

更快的 GPU：随着内核执行速度提升，可用于与 CPU 工作重叠的时间越来越少，CPU 开销因此愈发明显。

MoE complexity : MoE models add substantial complexity to FFN layers—routers, dispatch, Grouped GEMMs, and combine operations—yielding many small kernels beyond the GEMMs themselves and thus substantial CPU overhead.

MoE 的复杂性：MoE 模型给前馈网络（FFN）层带来了大量的复杂性——路由器、分发、分组 GEMM 与合并操作——除了 GEMM 本身之外还产生了许多小内核，从而带来可观的 CPU 开销。

Reduced-precision training : Extra quantization kernels, especially in fine-grained MoE where operation count scales with the number of experts.

低精度训练：额外的量化内核，尤其是在细粒度 MoE 中，算子数量随专家数量增长。

图： Figure 22 : Traditional execution (top) versus CUDA Graph execution (bottom).

图 22：传统执行（上）与 CUDA 图执行（下）的对比。

Figure 22 shows the large CPU overhead and the resulting GPU bubbles in a traditional execution pipeline, revealing large gaps between GPU kernels. This clearly indicates that the CPU cannot launch kernels fast enough to keep the GPU fully utilized, a phenomenon known as CPU overhead or host-boundedness. CUDA Graphs address this by capturing a sequence of GPU kernels into a replayable graph during an initial iteration [ 66 , 67 ] . Subsequent iterations issue a single Graph Launch call, largely bypassing per-op Python/framework overhead and per-kernel launch overhead, thereby reducing CPU-side latency.

图 22 展示了传统执行流水线中巨大的 CPU 开销以及由此产生的 GPU 空闲气泡，GPU 内核之间出现了很大的间隔。这清楚地表明，CPU 启动内核的速度不足以让 GPU 保持充分利用，这一现象被称为 CPU 开销或主机瓶颈（host-boundedness）。CUDA 图通过在初始迭代中将一系列 GPU 内核捕获为可重放的图来解决这一问题 [66, 67]。后续迭代只需发出一次图启动调用（Graph Launch），基本绕过了每个算子的 Python/框架开销和每个内核的启动开销，从而降低了 CPU 端的延迟。

However, CUDA Graphs require all captured operations to have static, predetermined shapes . Dynamic shapes or control flow cause graph capture to fail. This creates a fundamental tension with MoE, where token counts per expert vary dynamically. The next paragraph describes Megatron-Core’s two CUDA Graph modes—full CUDA Graphs and layer-wise CUDA Graphs—and how they address this dynamic-shape constraint in MoE.

然而，CUDA 图要求所有被捕获的算子都具有静态的、预先确定的形状。动态形状或控制流会导致图捕获失败。这与 MoE 形成了根本性的矛盾，因为每个专家的 token 数量是动态变化的。下一段介绍 Megatron-Core 的两种 CUDA 图模式——完整 CUDA 图（full CUDA Graphs）与逐层 CUDA 图（layer-wise CUDA Graphs）——以及它们如何应对 MoE 中的动态形状约束。

图： Figure 23 : Full versus layer-wise CUDA Graphs in one training iteration (three layers, two microbatches).

图 23：一次训练迭代中的完整 CUDA 图与逐层 CUDA 图对比（三层，两个微批次）。

As shown in figure 23 , two types of CUDA Graphs are supported in Megatron-Core: full CUDA Graphs and layer-wise CUDA Graphs. Full CUDA Graphs, also noted as full-iteration CUDA Graphs, capture the entire forward-backward pass into a single CUDA Graph. This is only supported for dense or MoE models using token dropping with padding , which means each expert has a fixed receiving capacity; tokens exceeding capacity are dropped, and inputs are padded to ensure constant tensor shapes. With all shapes static, the whole iteration can be graphed, maximizing CPU overhead reduction. As for dropless MoE models such as DeepSeek-V3, where no routed tokens can be dropped, full CUDA Graphs are not feasible because the number of tokens assigned to each expert varies dynamically based on routing decisions. Expert GEMM shapes change from iteration to iteration, violating the static-shape requirement. Additionally, device-host synchronization is required to retrieve per-expert token counts for dispatching. Section 4.3.7 describes our effort to overcome the dynamic dispatching and synchronization issues so that the full CUDA Graphs can also be applied to dropless MoE. Alternatively, here we introduce a simpler method based on layer-wise CUDA Graphs, called partial CUDA Graphs.

如图 23 所示，Megatron-Core 支持两种 CUDA 图：完整 CUDA 图和逐层 CUDA 图。完整 CUDA 图又称全迭代 CUDA 图（full-iteration CUDA Graphs），将整个前向-反向传播捕获到单个 CUDA 图中。这仅适用于采用带填充的 token 丢弃（token dropping with padding）的稠密模型或 MoE 模型，即每个专家具有固定的接收容量；超出容量的 token 被丢弃，并对输入进行填充以保证张量形状恒定。在所有形状均为静态的情况下，整个迭代都可以成图，从而最大程度地降低 CPU 开销。至于无丢弃 MoE 模型（如 DeepSeek-V3），其中路由 token 一律不得丢弃，完整 CUDA 图并不可行，因为分配给每个专家的 token 数量会随路由决策动态变化。专家 GEMM 的形状在迭代之间不断变化，违反了静态形状的要求。此外，还需要设备-主机同步来获取每个专家的 token 数量以进行分发。第 4.3.7 节介绍了我们为克服动态分发与同步问题所做的努力，以便完整 CUDA 图也能应用于无丢弃 MoE。作为替代方案，这里介绍一种基于逐层 CUDA 图的更简单方法，称为部分 CUDA 图（partial CUDA Graphs）。

图： Figure 24 : Partial CUDA Graphs capture static components (attention, shared experts, router, preprocessing) while leaving dynamic expert computation outside the graph.

图 24：部分 CUDA 图捕获静态组件（注意力、共享专家、路由器、预处理），同时将动态专家计算留在图外。

Layer-wise CUDA Graphs capture each transformer layer separately. As with full CUDA Graphs, capturing an entire transformer layer still runs into dynamic shapes in the MoE part. Partial CUDA Graphs avoid this by capturing only the static components of each layer while leaving dynamic parts outside the graph, yielding large performance gains for dropless MoE even without capturing the full model. In a dropless MoE layer:

逐层 CUDA 图逐个捕获每个 Transformer 层。与完整 CUDA 图一样，捕获整个 Transformer 层时仍会在 MoE 部分遇到动态形状问题。部分 CUDA 图通过仅捕获每层的静态组件、将动态部分留在图外来规避这一问题，即使不捕获整个模型，也能为无丢弃 MoE 带来大幅性能提升。在无丢弃 MoE 层中：

Static components (can be graphed):

静态组件（可成图）：

Attention layers (process a fixed number of tokens)

注意力层（处理固定数量的 token）

Router computation (fixed input/output shapes)

路由器计算（输入/输出形状固定）

Expert Parallelism (EP) preprocessing (static permutation metadata)

专家并行（Expert Parallelism，EP）预处理（静态置换元数据）

Shared experts (if present, process all tokens with fixed shapes)

共享专家（shared expert）（如存在，处理全部 token，形状固定）

MLP layers in dense transformer blocks

稠密 Transformer 块中的 MLP 层

Dynamic components (cannot be graphed):

动态组件（不可成图）：

Token dispatch (from fixed shape to dynamic shape)

token 分发（从固定形状到动态形状）

Expert GEMM operations (dynamic M dimension based on token assignment)

专家 GEMM 操作（M 维度随 token 分配动态变化）

Token combine (from dynamic shape to fixed shape)

token 合并（从动态形状到固定形状）

Partial CUDA Graphs capture the static portions of each layer independently, leaving routed expert computation to execute normally. As shown in figure 24 , the forward path of each transformer layer is split into several “scopes”, and the user can choose which scopes to graph. With the " attn+moe_router+moe_preprocess " scopes enabled, one graph captures all static components before the dispatching operation for that layer: attention, router (projection, top- $k$ selection, and auxiliary loss computation), and EP preprocessing (token metadata calculation and permutation). A shared expert, if present, is also captured in this graph. This approach eliminates CPU overhead while preserving correctness by allowing dynamic expert computation to execute with varying shapes. Despite not capturing the entire model, this achieves significant performance gains.

部分 CUDA 图独立捕获每层的静态部分，让路由专家计算正常执行。如图 24 所示，每个 Transformer 层的前向路径被划分为若干“作用域（scope）”，用户可以选择对哪些作用域成图。启用 "attn+moe_router+moe_preprocess" 作用域后，一个图捕获该层分发操作之前的所有静态组件：注意力、路由器（投影、top-$k$ 选择和辅助损失（auxiliary loss）计算）以及专家并行预处理（token 元数据计算与置换）。共享专家（如存在）也被捕获在该图中。这种方法在消除 CPU 开销的同时，通过允许动态专家计算以变化的形状执行来保证正确性。尽管没有捕获整个模型，这仍带来了显著的性能提升。

图： Figure 25 : Transformer layer forward pass: without (upper) and with (lower) partial CUDA Graphs. CPU overhead is largely eliminated for static components.

图 25：Transformer 层前向传播：不使用（上）与使用（下）部分 CUDA 图。静态组件的 CPU 开销基本消除。

Megatron-Core has been extensively adapted so that CUDA Graphs work correctly with key features like Multi-Token Prediction (MTP), EP communication overlapping, fine-grained offloading and checkpointing, and flexible PP layouts. Nsight Systems profiling with partial CUDA Graphs enabled shows greatly reduced CPU overhead (figure 25 ). The remaining CPU overhead is primarily confined to the region between token dispatch and combine operations, which cannot be graphed due to their dynamic nature. See Section 4.3.7 for more details on how to address this remaining bottleneck by making the whole layer graphable.

Megatron-Core 经过了广泛的适配，使 CUDA 图能够与多 token 预测（Multi-Token Prediction，MTP）、专家并行通信重叠、细粒度卸载（offloading）与检查点（checkpoint）以及灵活的流水线并行（pipeline parallelism，PP）布局等关键特性正确协同工作。启用部分 CUDA 图后的 Nsight Systems 性能分析显示，CPU 开销大幅降低（图 25）。剩余的 CPU 开销主要集中在 token 分发与合并操作之间的区域，由于其动态特性，该区域无法成图。关于如何通过使整个层可成图来解决这一剩余瓶颈，详见第 4.3.7 节。

CUDA Graphs add memory overhead from three sources:

CUDA 图从三个方面带来内存开销：

Graph structure : Memory for storing the graph topology (typically negligible).

图结构：存储图拓扑的内存（通常可忽略不计）。

Separate memory pools : Graphed and non-graphed operations need separate PyTorch memory pools and cannot share memory. With partial CUDA Graphs, a single transformer layer needs two pools: one for graphed work and one for non-graphed work.

独立的内存池：成图与非成图操作需要各自独立的 PyTorch 内存池，无法共享内存。使用部分 CUDA 图时，单个 Transformer 层需要两个内存池：一个用于成图操作，一个用于非成图操作。

Static buffers : Each graph needs dedicated input/output static buffers that, once allocated, are taken out of PyTorch’s memory allocator.

静态缓冲区：每个图都需要专用的输入/输出静态缓冲区，一旦分配，就不再归 PyTorch 内存分配器管理。

If not carefully optimized, partial CUDA Graphs would incur much higher memory overhead from the above three sources. Megatron-Core and Transformer Engine implement several optimizations to minimize this overhead:

如果不加以精心优化，部分 CUDA 图会因上述三个来源产生高得多的内存开销。Megatron-Core 与 Transformer Engine 实施了多项优化来尽可能降低这一开销：

Reducing the number of graphs. With pipeline parallelism (PP), each microbatch requires a dedicated CUDA Graph. The reason is that if microbatches share a graph, microbatch $i+1$ ’s forward pass will overwrite the saved context before microbatch $i$ ’s backward pass executes, causing memory corruption (figure 26 ). This results in $L\times M\times 2$ graphs in total (where $L$ = layers per GPU, $M$ = microbatches, $\times 2$ for forward/backward).

减少图的数量。在使用流水线并行（PP）时，每个微批次（micro-batch）都需要一个专用的 CUDA 图。原因在于，如果微批次共享一个图，微批次 $i+1$ 的前向传播会在微批次 $i$ 的反向传播执行之前覆盖已保存的上下文，导致内存损坏（图 26）。这总共会产生 $L\times M\times 2$ 个图（其中 $L$ = 每 GPU 的层数，$M$ = 微批次数量，$\times 2$ 对应前向/反向）。

On the contrary, if PP is not used, microbatches can share the same graph, reducing the count to $L\times 2$ . To enable graph sharing, an is_first_microbatch GPU flag is introduced to control microbatch-specific behaviors within the shared graph, e.g., quantization that runs only on the first microbatch. Setting this flag to 0 or 1 before replay causes the captured quantization kernel to be skipped or executed as needed.

相反，如果不使用流水线并行，微批次可以共享同一个图，图的数量降至 $L\times 2$。为了实现图共享，引入了一个 is_first_microbatch GPU 标志，用于控制共享图内与微批次相关的行为，例如仅在第一个微批次上运行的量化。在重放前将该标志设为 0 或 1，可使被捕获的量化内核按需跳过或执行。

图： Figure 26 : Why Pipeline Parallelism prevents CUDA Graphs from being shared across microbatches. With PP (top): Execution is interleaved—multiple forward passes run before any backward pass. If microbatches share a graph, F_mb1 overwrites saved context of F_mb0 before B_mb0 uses it, causing memory corruption. Each microbatch needs its own graph ( $L\times M\times 2$ graphs total). Without PP (bottom): Execution is sequential—each microbatch completes (forward+backward) before the next starts. Context is consumed before being overwritten, so microbatches can safely share graphs (only $L\times 2$ graphs total).

图 26：流水线并行为何会阻止 CUDA 图在微批次之间共享。使用流水线并行时（上）：执行是交错的——多个前向传播在任何反向传播之前运行。如果微批次共享一个图，F_mb1 会在 B_mb0 使用 F_mb0 已保存的上下文之前将其覆盖，导致内存损坏。每个微批次都需要自己的图（共 $L\times M\times 2$ 个图）。不使用流水线并行时（下）：执行是顺序的——每个微批次完成（前向+反向）后下一个才开始。上下文在被覆盖之前已被消耗，因此微批次可以安全地共享图（仅需 $L\times 2$ 个图）。

Pool sharing. Although graphed and non-graphed operations require separate memory pools, all graphs can share one pool when captured in execution order. Transformer Engine’s make_graphed_callables() API accepts an _order argument that specifies the inter-microbatch scheduling, allowing sequential capture with correct pool sharing.

内存池共享。虽然成图与非成图操作需要独立的内存池，但如果按执行顺序捕获，所有图可以共享一个内存池。Transformer Engine 的 make_graphed_callables() API 接受一个 _order 参数，该参数指定微批次间的调度，从而允许按顺序捕获并实现正确的内存池共享。

Buffer reuse. Static input/output buffers can be reused across graphs according to the PP execution order. Once a microbatch finishes its backward pass of a layer, its forward buffers and backward input buffer become available for the next forward and backward graphs to reuse. Only the backward output buffer must be kept until the data is sent to the next PP stage.

缓冲区复用。静态输入/输出缓冲区可以根据流水线并行的执行顺序在图之间复用。一旦某个微批次完成某层的反向传播，其前向缓冲区与反向输入缓冲区便可供下一个前向图与反向图复用。只有反向输出缓冲区必须保留，直到数据发送到下一个流水线并行阶段。

With these memory optimizations, we achieve 10% end-to-end speedup with about 7 GB extra memory on DeepSeek-V3 GB200 training.

通过这些内存优化，我们在 DeepSeek-V3 GB200 训练上实现了 10% 的端到端加速，额外内存仅约 7 GB。

Full CUDA Graphs do not support dropless MoE. Partial CUDA Graphs eliminate CPU overhead for static components, but the dynamic expert computation region remains outside the graph . Is there any way we could make CUDA Graphs cover the dynamic part? There are at least two challenges we must solve to achieve this:

完整 CUDA 图不支持无丢弃 MoE。部分 CUDA 图消除了静态组件的 CPU 开销，但动态专家计算区域仍在图外。有没有办法让 CUDA 图覆盖动态部分？要实现这一点，至少需要解决两个挑战：

Dynamic shapes require host–device synchronization : The host must query per-expert token counts from the GPU to launch token dispatching and Grouped GEMMs.

动态形状需要主机-设备同步：主机必须从 GPU 查询每个专家的 token 数量，才能启动 token 分发和分组 GEMM。

Memory allocation requires known buffer sizes : Worst-case buffer allocation wastes memory; actual-size allocation requires synchronization.

内存分配需要已知的缓冲区大小：按最坏情况分配缓冲区会浪费内存；而按实际大小分配则需要同步。

The next subsection presents how Megatron-Core achieves full CUDA Graphs coverage for dropless MoE through three complementary techniques: sync-free kernels, ECHO, and Paged Stashing.

下一小节介绍 Megatron-Core 如何通过三项互补技术——无同步内核、动态专家克隆（ECHO）与分页暂存（Paged Stashing）——实现无丢弃 MoE 的完整 CUDA 图覆盖。

4.3.7. Full CUDA Graphs Coverage for Dropless MoE via Sync-Free Kernels, ECHO, and Paged Stashing（4.3.7. 通过无同步内核、ECHO 与分页暂存实现无丢弃 MoE 的完整 CUDA 图覆盖）

While kernel fusion and CUDA Graphs help reduce host overhead, dropless MoE introduces a new challenge: dynamic tensor shapes require host-device synchronization, preventing CUDA Graphs from covering expert computation.

尽管内核融合与 CUDA 图有助于降低主机开销，无丢弃 MoE 却带来了新的挑战：动态张量形状需要主机-设备同步，从而使 CUDA 图无法覆盖专家计算。

In dropless MoE, token counts received by each EP rank vary dynamically based on routing decisions. The router generates this shape information on the GPU, but the host needs it to launch subsequent operations and allocate memory. Obtaining this information requires a device-to-host copy and synchronization, placing the CPU on the critical path and preventing CUDA Graphs capturing of the dynamic portions. To enable sync-free MoE for dropless training with dynamic routing, there are two fundamental challenges:

在无丢弃 MoE 中，每个 EP rank 接收到的 token 数量随路由决策动态变化。路由器在 GPU 上生成这些形状信息，但主机需要这些信息来启动后续操作并分配内存。获取这些信息需要一次设备到主机的拷贝与同步，使 CPU 处于关键路径上，并阻碍 CUDA 图捕获动态部分。为了在动态路由的无丢弃训练中实现无同步 MoE，存在两个根本性挑战：

Challenge 1: Kernel launch without knowing the actual problem size. Conventionally, many GPU operators assume that (dynamic) shape information is available on the host. The host-side code determines launch configurations, such as grid size and tile size, as well as the amount of work the kernel must perform based on the shape information obtained at run time. In dropless MoE, this creates a host-device synchronization point: the host must query per-expert token counts from the device before launching Grouped GEMM or communication kernels. To tackle this issue, the GPU kernels handling dynamic shapes need to be redesigned. We developed device-initiated GPU kernels , including device-initiated Grouped GEMM and sync-free dispatch with HybridEP.

挑战 1：不知道实际问题规模时的内核启动。传统上，许多 GPU 算子假定（动态）形状信息在主机上可用。主机端代码根据运行时获得的形状信息确定启动配置，如网格尺寸（grid size）与分块尺寸（tile size），以及内核必须执行的工作量。在无丢弃 MoE 中，这形成了一个主机-设备同步点：主机必须先从设备查询每个专家的 token 数量，然后才能启动分组 GEMM 或通信内核。为解决这一问题，需要重新设计处理动态形状的 GPU 内核。我们开发了设备端发起的 GPU 内核（device-initiated GPU kernels），包括设备端发起的分组 GEMM 与结合混合专家并行（HybridEP）的无同步分发。

Challenge 2: Memory allocation without knowing the actual size. Sync-free execution means the size of buffers needs to be pre-determined on the host. To avoid token overflow, buffers often need to be over-sized, e.g., based on the worst-case size to accommodate the case where all tokens are routed to the same expert. This over-provisioning, however, leads to severe memory fragmentation: if the actual token distribution is balanced, the vast majority of the preallocated memory remains unused, yet it cannot be reclaimed by other operations within the graph. The worst-case buffer potentially requires $O(\text{EP_size})$ times more memory than the actual working set.

挑战 2：不知道实际大小时的内存分配。无同步执行意味着缓冲区的大小需要在主机上预先确定。为避免 token 溢出，缓冲区往往需要超额配置，例如按最坏情况的大小配置，以容纳所有 token 都被路由到同一专家的情形。然而，这种超额配置会导致严重的内存碎片化（memory fragmentation）：如果实际 token 分布是均衡的，绝大部分预分配内存仍处于未使用状态，却无法被图内的其他操作回收。最坏情况下的缓冲区可能需要比实际工作集多 $O(\text{EP_size})$ 倍的内存。

This memory fragmentation can be mitigated through two complementary strategies: reducing load imbalance so that worst-case provisioning is closer to actual usage, and dynamic memory management within the CUDA Graph to reclaim unused buffer space. ECHO employs the first strategy by dynamically cloning popular experts on underutilized ranks, reducing the variance in per-rank token counts and thus the gap between worst-case and average memory requirements. Paged Stashing adopts the second strategy by enabling fine-grained memory management within CUDA Graph, allowing unused portions of preallocated buffers to be repurposed for other operations. ECHO and Paged Stashing are detailed below.

这种内存碎片化可以通过两种互补策略来缓解：减少负载不均衡（load imbalance），使最坏情况配置更接近实际用量；以及在 CUDA 图内进行动态内存管理，以回收未使用的缓冲区空间。ECHO 采用第一种策略，通过在利用率不足的 rank 上动态克隆热门专家，减少每个 rank 的 token 数量方差，从而缩小最坏情况与平均内存需求之间的差距。分页暂存采用第二种策略，通过在 CUDA 图内实现细粒度内存管理，使预分配缓冲区中未使用的部分可被重新用于其他操作。下面详细介绍 ECHO 与分页暂存。

To eliminate the mandatory host-device synchronization required by conventional host-initiated GPU operators, kernels must be device-initiated . This requires three things:

为了消除传统主机端发起（host-initiated）的 GPU 算子所必需的主机-设备同步，内核必须是设备端发起的。这需要做到三点：

The GPU kernel needs to read shape information from GPU memory and use it to guide the computation.

GPU 内核需要从 GPU 内存中读取形状信息，并用它来指导计算。

The GPU kernel needs to decouple the actual amount of work it performs, which is only known at runtime, from its static launch configuration.

GPU 内核需要将其实际执行的工作量（仅在运行时才知道）与其静态启动配置解耦。

The GPU kernel needs to skip unnecessary computation, such as operations on padded data.

GPU 内核需要跳过不必要的计算，例如对填充数据的操作。

To satisfy these requirements, existing kernels must be redesigned. The Grouped GEMM kernel and HybridEP kernel serve as two concrete examples below.

为满足这些要求，必须重新设计现有内核。下面以分组 GEMM 内核与 HybridEP 内核作为两个具体示例。

Device-Initiated Grouped GEMM. As discussed in Section 4.3.2 , TE offers two Grouped GEMM implementations: the multi-stream cuBLASLt GEMMs and the CUTLASS Grouped GEMM. Both are host-initiated. They require a CPU-side list of per-expert token counts to determine each GEMM’s shape and launch configuration, creating a host-device synchronization barrier. To eliminate this, TE introduces device-initiated Grouped GEMM, which reads shape information directly from device memory. Two implementations are provided:

设备端发起的分组 GEMM。如第 4.3.2 节所述，TE（Transformer Engine）提供两种分组 GEMM 实现：多流 cuBLASLt GEMM 与 CUTLASS 分组 GEMM。两者都是主机端发起的。它们需要一份 CPU 端的每个专家 token 数量列表来确定每个 GEMM 的形状与启动配置，从而形成主机-设备同步屏障。为消除这一屏障，TE 引入了设备端发起的分组 GEMM，它直接从设备内存读取形状信息。共提供两种实现：

cuBLASLt Grouped GEMM. Since CUDA 13.1, cuBLASLt Grouped GEMM supports passing matrix shapes as device arrays. This implementation includes built-in heuristics for selecting optimal kernel configurations, now covering various precisions and scaling modes across recent CUDA releases.

cuBLASLt 分组 GEMM。自 CUDA 13.1 起，cuBLASLt 分组 GEMM 支持将矩阵形状作为设备端数组传入。该实现内置了用于选择最优内核配置的启发式策略，目前已覆盖近期 CUDA 版本中的多种精度与缩放模式。

cuteDSL Grouped GEMM with Fused Activation and Quantization. In the cuteDSL-based implementation, the SwiGLU activation can be fused into the epilogue stage, and for FP8 training, quantization can also be fused. While current support is limited to specific precisions and fusion patterns, ongoing development continues to expand coverage across new configurations.

带融合激活与量化的 cuteDSL 分组 GEMM。在基于 cuteDSL 的实现中，SwiGLU 激活可以融合到收尾（epilogue）阶段，对于 FP8 训练，量化也可以融合。虽然目前的支持仅限于特定精度与融合模式，但持续的开发正在不断扩大对新配置的覆盖。

Sync-Free Dispatch with HybridEP. HybridEP, introduced in Section 4.2.2 , provides an efficient implementation of MoE communication and also plays an important role in sync-free MoE. After each dispatch and permutation, the number of tokens received by a given rank is dynamic, normally requiring synchronization to obtain buffer sizes. By estimating an upper bound and passing it to HybridEP, the dispatcher can pre-allocate output buffers according to this bound, eliminating all synchronizations at the cost of additional GPU memory.

结合 HybridEP 的无同步分发。HybridEP 在第 4.2.2 节中介绍过，它提供了 MoE 通信的高效实现，在无同步 MoE 中也发挥着重要作用。每次分发与置换之后，某个 rank 接收到的 token 数量是动态的，通常需要同步来获取缓冲区大小。通过估计一个上界并将其传递给 HybridEP，分发器可以根据该上界预分配输出缓冲区，从而消除所有同步，代价是额外的 GPU 内存。

Even with device-initiated kernels, worst-case buffer allocation wastes memory. Two complementary strategies address this: ECHO reduces load imbalance so worst-case provisioning approaches actual usage, while Paged Stashing enables fine-grained memory management to reclaim unused buffer space.

即使使用设备端发起的内核，按最坏情况分配缓冲区仍会浪费内存。两种互补策略可以解决这一问题：ECHO 减少负载不均衡，使最坏情况配置接近实际用量；分页暂存则实现细粒度内存管理，以回收未使用的缓冲区空间。

Load imbalance is inherent in MoE: popular experts receive far more tokens than others, creating two problems. First, EP ranks hosting hot experts become compute bottlenecks, causing other ranks to wait at synchronization points. Second, high load variance means worst-case buffer provisioning wastes significant memory compared to actual usage. ECHO addresses both by dynamically cloning hot experts to spare slots on underutilized ranks.

负载不均衡是 MoE 固有的问题：热门专家接收到的 token 远多于其他专家，这会造成两个问题。第一，托管热门专家的 EP rank 成为计算瓶颈，导致其他 rank 在同步点等待。第二，高负载方差意味着按最坏情况配置缓冲区会比实际用量浪费大量内存。ECHO 通过将热门专家动态克隆到利用率不足的 rank 上的空闲槽位来同时解决这两个问题。

figure 27 illustrates the ECHO workflow. In the forward pass, the ECHO planner identifies hot experts and generates two outputs: a hot expert map indicating which experts to clone to which spare slots, and an updated routing map redirecting overflow tokens to clones. Expert Dispatch copies hot expert weights to spare slots via HybridEP-based sync-free communication; Token Dispatch routes tokens to both home and cloned experts; expert computation proceeds on all experts. In the backward pass, Expert Gradient Dispatch collects gradients from clones and reduces them to home experts, ensuring consistency. Cloned experts are discarded after computation to save memory.

图 27 展示了 ECHO 的工作流程。在前向传播中，ECHO 规划器（planner）识别热门专家并生成两个输出：热门专家映射图（hot expert map），指明将哪些专家克隆到哪些空闲槽位；以及更新后的路由图（routing map），将溢出的 token 重定向到克隆专家。专家分发（Expert Dispatch）通过基于 HybridEP 的无同步通信将热门专家权重复制到空闲槽位；token 分发（Token Dispatch）将 token 路由到归属专家与克隆专家；专家计算在所有专家上进行。在反向传播中，专家梯度分发（Expert Gradient Dispatch）收集克隆专家的梯度并将其归约回归属专家，以保证一致性。克隆专家在计算完成后即被丢弃，以节省内存。

图： Figure 27 : ECHO workflow for forward and backward passes. The planner generates routing and hot expert maps. Expert Dispatch clones hot expert weights to spare slots; Expert Gradient Dispatch reduces gradients back to home experts.

图 27：ECHO 前向与反向传播的工作流程。规划器生成路由图与热门专家映射图。专家分发将热门专家权重复制到空闲槽位；专家梯度分发将梯度归约回归属专家。

Cloning experts for training is much more expensive than for inference: each cloned expert requires weight communication during forward and gradient reduction during backward to ensure consistency. Cloning all hot experts to all spare slots would maximize load balance but introduce excessive communication overhead. The goal of the ECHO planner is to minimize the number of expert clones while achieving sufficient load balance. It computes the spillover for each expert (tokens exceeding the average load per EP rank) and the spare capacity on each rank (available capacity below average). Using a bin-packing algorithm, the planner efficiently matches spillover tokens to spare capacity, determining which specific experts to clone and to which ranks, producing the hot expert map and routing map with minimal cloning overhead.
