Some MoE architectures (DeepSeek-V2/V3 [ 17 , 6 ] , Qwen [ 32 ] ) include a shared expert that processes all tokens regardless of routing, providing consistent baseline capacity across all tokens (figure 40 ). When overlap is enabled ( --moe-shared-expert-overlap ), shared expert computation runs in parallel with the all-to-all communication and routed expert computation, hiding its latency behind the dispatch-compute-combine pipeline.

一些混合专家架构（DeepSeek-V2/V3 [ 17 , 6 ]、Qwen [ 32 ]）包含一个共享专家（shared expert），它处理所有 token 而不考虑路由，为所有 token 提供一致的基础容量（图 40）。启用重叠后（--moe-shared-expert-overlap），共享专家计算与全互联通信及被路由专家计算并行运行，将其延迟隐藏在分发（dispatch）-计算-合并（combine）流水线之后。

图： Figure 40 : Shared expert architecture in Megatron-Core MoE. The shared expert processes all tokens while routed experts process only their assigned tokens. When overlap is enabled, shared expert computation runs in parallel with the token dispatch/combine communication, hiding its latency.

图 40：Megatron-Core 混合专家中的共享专家架构。共享专家处理全部 token，而被路由专家仅处理分配给它们的 token。启用重叠后，共享专家计算与 token 分发/合并通信并行运行，从而隐藏其延迟。

7.3. Latent MoE（7.3. 隐式 MoE）

In standard MoE, all2all communication dispatches tokens at the full hidden dimension $d$ , and each expert is parameterized by weight matrices in $\mathbb{R}^{m\times d}$ and $\mathbb{R}^{d\times m}$ . LatentMoE [ 19 ] reduces both costs by inserting a shared down-projection $W_{\downarrow}\in\mathbb{R}^{\ell\times d}$ before dispatch and an up-projection $W_{\uparrow}\in\mathbb{R}^{d\times\ell}$ after combine, where $\ell<d$ is the latent dimension. Routing still operates on the full hidden dimension to preserve routing quality; each routed expert operates entirely in the compressed latent space; shared experts remain at dimension $d$ . The layer output becomes:

在标准混合专家中，全互联通信以完整隐藏维度 $d$ 分发 token，每个专家由权重矩阵 $\mathbb{R}^{m\times d}$ 与 $\mathbb{R}^{d\times m}$ 参数化。隐式 MoE（Latent MoE）[ 19 ] 通过在分发前插入共享下投影 $W_{\downarrow}\in\mathbb{R}^{\ell\times d}$，并在合并后插入上投影 $W_{\uparrow}\in\mathbb{R}^{d\times\ell}$，同时降低了这两项成本，其中 $\ell<d$ 为隐维度。路由仍在完整隐藏维度上进行，以保持路由质量；每个被路由专家完全在压缩后的隐空间中运行；共享专家保持在维度 $d$。层输出变为：

$\text{output}(\mathbf{x})=W_{\uparrow}\cdot\Bigl(\sum_{i\in\mathcal{T}{K,E}}p{i}\,E_{i}(W_{\downarrow}\cdot\mathbf{x};\,\ell)\Bigr)+\sum_{j}E_{j}^{\text{shared}}(\mathbf{x};\,d).$

The compression ratio $\alpha=d/\ell$ reduces all2all communication volume by a factor of $\alpha$ (tokens are dispatched at dimension $\ell$ instead of $d$ ) and per-expert weight size by the same factor (expert matrices shrink from $\mathbb{R}^{m\times d}$ to $\mathbb{R}^{m\times\ell}$ ). Elango et al. [ 19 ] define two ways to exploit these savings. In $\ell\text{-MoE}{\text{eff}}$ , the total expert count $E$ is scaled by $\alpha$ while top- $K$ is unchanged, preserving baseline accuracy at reduced inference cost. In $\ell\text{-MoE}{\text{acc}}$ (recommended), both $E$ and top- $K$ are scaled by $\alpha$ , which restores inference cost to the standard-MoE level but exponentially expands the combinatorial space of expert selections ( $\binom{\alpha E}{\alpha K}\geq\binom{E}{K}^{\alpha}$ ), yielding higher accuracy at iso-cost. At scales up to 95B parameters, $\ell\text{-MoE}_{\text{acc}}$ consistently outperforms standard MoE in accuracy per FLOP and per parameter. The architecture has been adopted by NVIDIA’s Nemotron-3 Super and Ultra models.

压缩比 $\alpha=d/\ell$ 将全互联通信量降低 $\alpha$ 倍（token 以维度 $\ell$ 而非 $d$ 分发），并将每个专家的权重规模缩小相同倍数（专家矩阵从 $\mathbb{R}^{m\times d}$ 缩小到 $\mathbb{R}^{m\times\ell}$）。Elango 等人 [ 19 ] 定义了两种利用这些节省的方式。在 $\ell\text{-MoE}{\text{eff}}$ 中，专家总数 $E$ 按 $\alpha$ 扩展，而 top- $K$ 不变，在降低推理成本的同时保持基线精度。在 $\ell\text{-MoE}{\text{acc}}$（推荐）中，$E$ 与 top- $K$ 均按 $\alpha$ 扩展，这将推理成本恢复到标准混合专家水平，但指数级扩大了专家选择的组合空间（$\binom{\alpha E}{\alpha K}\geq\binom{E}{K}^{\alpha}$），从而在相同成本下获得更高的精度。在高达 95B 参数的规模下，$\ell\text{-MoE}_{\text{acc}}$ 在每 FLOPs 精度与每参数精度上始终优于标准混合专家。该架构已被 NVIDIA 的 Nemotron-3 Super 与 Ultra 模型采用。

Implementation. LatentMoE is enabled via --moe-latent-size , which sets $\ell$ . The MoELayer instantiates two TELinear projections: fc1_latent_proj (down-projection in the preprocess step, after routing but before dispatch) and fc2_latent_proj (up-projection in the postprocess step, after combine). Expert backends ( TEGroupedMLP , SequentialMLP ) automatically adapt their input and output dimensions to $\ell$ .

实现。隐式 MoE 通过 --moe-latent-size 启用，该参数设置 $\ell$。MoELayer 实例化两个 TELinear 投影：fc1_latent_proj（在预处理步骤中进行下投影，在路由之后、分发之前）与 fc2_latent_proj（在后处理步骤中进行上投影，在合并之后）。专家后端（TEGroupedMLP、SequentialMLP）自动将其输入与输出维度适配为 $\ell$。

7.4. Distributed Checkpoint（7.4. 分布式检查点）

Traditional checkpointing tightly couples saved model state with the specific parallelism configuration, requiring complex offline conversion when changing TP, EP, PP, or other parallelism settings. Our distributed checkpoint library solves this through parallelism-agnostic checkpointing with automatic resharding.

传统检查点（checkpoint）保存将保存的模型状态与特定的并行配置紧密耦合，在更改张量并行、专家并行（expert parallelism，EP）、流水线并行或其他并行设置时需要复杂的离线转换。我们的分布式检查点库通过支持自动重分片的并行无关检查点保存解决了这一问题。

The core abstraction is the ShardedTensor descriptor, which encodes each local tensor’s global shape, offset, and sharding pattern. During saving, each rank independently writes its local shard (Fully Parallel Saving), eliminating coordinator bottlenecks. During loading, each rank determines which portions of global tensors it needs based on the new sharding specification and reads only those slices.

核心抽象是 ShardedTensor 描述符，它编码每个局部张量的全局形状、偏移与分片模式。在保存期间，每个 rank 独立写入其局部分片（完全并行保存），消除了协调器瓶颈。在加载期间，每个 rank 根据新的分片规范确定其所需的全局张量部分，并仅读取这些切片。

This enables any-to-any parallelism reconfiguration: a checkpoint saved with $\text{TP}=2$ , $\text{EP}=4$ can be loaded with $\text{TP}=4$ , $\text{EP}=8$ without offline conversion. The library supports Zarr (default) and PyTorch Distributed [ 53 ] storage backends.

这使得任意到任意的并行重配置成为可能：以 $\text{TP}=2$、$\text{EP}=4$ 保存的检查点可以以 $\text{TP}=4$、$\text{EP}=8$ 加载，无需离线转换。该库支持 Zarr（默认）与 PyTorch Distributed [ 53 ] 存储后端。

7.5. Flexible Asymmetric Virtual Pipeline Parallelism（7.5. 灵活非对称虚拟流水线并行）

Interleaved pipeline parallelism [ 94 ] divides each physical pipeline stage into multiple virtual stages with interleaved scheduling, mitigating pipeline bubbles. Traditional VPP requires uniform layer distribution (e.g., a 24-layer model with $\text{PP}=4$ , $\text{VPP}=2$ distributes layers as $[6,6,6,6]$ ). However, MoE models exhibit substantial workload heterogeneity: MoE layers, dense layers, embedding, loss, and specialized layers like MTP have significantly different computational costs.

交错流水线并行 [ 94 ] 将每个物理流水线阶段划分为多个虚拟阶段，并采用交错调度，从而缓解流水线气泡。传统虚拟流水线并行（Virtual Pipeline Parallelism，VPP）要求均匀的层分布（例如，一个 24 层模型在 $\text{PP}=4$、$\text{VPP}=2$ 下按 $[6,6,6,6]$ 分布层）。然而，混合专家模型表现出显著的工作负载异构性：混合专家层、稠密层、嵌入层、损失层以及多 token 预测（Multi-Token Prediction，MTP）等专用层的计算成本差异显著。

We introduce Flexible Asymmetric VPP , which allows different numbers and types of layers per virtual stage. Consider DeepSeek-V3 with 61 decoder layers and 1 MTP layer at $\text{PP}=16$ , $\text{VPP}=2$ (Table 10 ). The initial rank combines the lightweight embedding with 3 dense decoders (matching the cost of 2 MoE layers). Most ranks hold 2 MoE decoders per stage. The final ranks strategically place the heavy MTP layer and lightweight loss layer to balance workload.

我们引入灵活非对称虚拟流水线并行，它允许每个虚拟阶段具有不同数量与类型的层。考虑 DeepSeek-V3 的 61 个解码器层与 1 个多 token 预测层，在 $\text{PP}=16$、$\text{VPP}=2$ 下（表 10）。初始 rank 将轻量级嵌入层与 3 个稠密解码器结合（与 2 个混合专家层的成本相当）。大多数 rank 在每个阶段持有 2 个混合专家解码器。最后的 rank 策略性地放置重型多 token 预测层与轻量级损失层，以平衡工作负载。

This approach enables VPP for models with arbitrary layer counts, achieves true load balancing by accounting for per-layer computational costs, and provides flexible placement for specialized layers.

这种方法使虚拟流水线并行适用于任意层数的模型，通过考虑每层计算成本实现真正的负载均衡，并为专用层提供灵活的放置。

表： Table 10 : Layer distribution for DeepSeek-V3 with flexible asymmetric VPP ( $\text{PP}=16$ , $\text{VPP}=2$ ).

表 10：DeepSeek-V3 在灵活非对称虚拟流水线并行下的层分布（$\text{PP}=16$，$\text{VPP}=2$）。

PP 序号 VPP 序号 0 VPP 序号 1
0 嵌入层 + 3 $\times$ 解码器层 2 $\times$ 解码器层
1–13 2 $\times$ 解码器层 2 $\times$ 解码器层
14 2 $\times$ 解码器层 MTP
15 2 $\times$ 解码器层 损失

This flexible asymmetric approach (figure 41 ) delivers several key advantages: (1) it enables VPP for models with arbitrary layer counts and compositions, removing artificial constraints on model architecture design; (2) it achieves true computational load balancing by allowing fine-grained control over layer distribution, accounting for the vast differences in computational cost between layer types; and (3) it provides flexible placement strategies for specialized layers (MTP, encoder-decoder structures, etc.), enabling diverse model architectures to fully exploit the latency-hiding benefits of pipeline parallelism. Practitioners can design layouts that distribute computationally expensive layers across ranks to avoid memory pressure and minimize pipeline bubbles.

这种灵活非对称方法（图 41）带来了几个关键优势：(1) 它使虚拟流水线并行适用于任意层数与组成的模型，消除了模型架构设计中的人为约束；(2) 它通过对层分布的细粒度控制实现真正的计算负载均衡，同时考虑了不同层类型之间计算成本的巨大差异；(3) 它为专用层（多 token 预测、编码器-解码器结构等）提供灵活的放置策略，使各种模型架构能够充分利用流水线并行的延迟隐藏优势。从业者可以设计将计算昂贵的层分布到各 rank 的布局，以避免内存压力并最小化流水线气泡。

图： Figure 41 : Flexible Pipeline Parallel Placement.

图 41：灵活流水线并行布局。

7.6. Upcycling（7.6. 模型升级转换）

Upcycling converts a pre-trained dense model into a sparse MoE architecture, expanding model capacity without retraining from scratch [ 95 , 96 , 97 ] . We support virtual group initialization and expert weight scaling to enable seamless adaptation of dense checkpoints into fine-grained MoE models. The approach employs softmax-then-topK routing, which routes tokens through a selective subset of experts for increased expressivity at constant or reduced inference cost, as illustrated in figure 42 .

模型升级转换（upcycling）将预训练稠密模型转换为稀疏混合专家架构，在无需从头重新训练的情况下扩展模型容量 [ 95 , 96 , 97 ]。我们支持虚拟组初始化与专家权重缩放，使稠密检查点能够无缝适配为细粒度混合专家模型。该方法采用 softmax-then-topK 路由，通过选择性专家子集路由 token，在恒定或降低的推理成本下提高表达能力，如图 42 所示。

图： Figure 42 : An example of granular upcycling a dense layer into E2G2T2 fine-grained MoE. E2G2T2 denotes 4 experts, top 2, with half intermediate size. (1) We shard MLP weights in the intermediate dimension ( $4h\rightarrow 2h$ ) then duplicate the shards. (2) We initialize half the router weights then duplicate them. This ensures Top2 always selects one of each MLP shard so MoE output is the same as the dense model at the start of training.

图 42：将稠密层细粒度模型升级转换为 E2G2T2 细粒度混合专家的示例。E2G2T2 表示 4 个专家、取前 2 个、中间维度减半。(1) 我们在中间维度上对 MLP 权重做分片（$4h\rightarrow 2h$），然后复制这些分片。(2) 我们初始化一半路由器权重，然后复制它们。这保证了 Top2 总是选中每个 MLP 分片中的一个，从而使混合专家输出在训练开始时与稠密模型相同。

7.7. Multi-Token Prediction（7.7. 多 token 预测）

Multi-token prediction (MTP) optimizes a model to predict multiple consecutive future tokens at each position, densifying the supervision signal [ 98 , 6 ] . Unlike parallel independent predictions, MTP maintains causal dependencies between predictions through hidden state transitions, accelerating convergence and improving generation quality. During inference, the model reverts to standard single-token prediction for deployment compatibility.

多 token 预测优化模型，使其在每个位置预测多个连续的未来 token，从而使监督信号更密集 [ 98 , 6 ]。与并行独立预测不同，多 token 预测通过隐藏状态转移保持预测之间的因果依赖，从而加速收敛并提高生成质量。在推理期间，模型恢复为标准单 token 预测，以保证部署兼容性。

We integrate MTP with flexible pipeline parallelism, allowing MTP layers to be placed strategically within the VPP layout for balanced workload distribution.

我们将多 token 预测与灵活流水线并行集成，使多 token 预测层能够在虚拟流水线并行布局中策略性放置，以实现均衡的工作负载分布。

7.8. Muon Optimizer（7.8. Muon 优化器）

Unlike traditional optimizers such as AdamW that perform element-wise updates, the Muon optimizer introduces a matrix-aware approach by orthogonalizing entire weight matrices [ 99 ] . This improves the conditioning of optimization trajectories and significantly reduces training steps compared to AdamW.

与执行逐元素更新的 AdamW 等传统优化器不同，Muon 优化器（Muon optimizer）通过正交化整个权重矩阵引入了一种矩阵感知方法 [ 99 ]。这改善了优化轨迹的条件，并与 AdamW 相比显著减少了训练步数。

Our integration provides production-ready support for large-scale distributed training with several key advantages: (1) full support for split query-key-value (QKV) weight layouts, enabling efficient orthogonalization even when attention projection matrices are stored as separate tensors; (2) seamless integration with the distributed optimizer, allowing optimizer states to be sharded across data-parallel ranks while maintaining correct orthogonalization semantics; and (3) CPU offloading for Muon’s orthogonalization buffers when GPU memory is constrained.

我们的集成方案为大规模分布式训练提供了生产就绪的支持，具有几个关键优势：(1) 完全支持拆分查询-键-值（QKV）权重布局，即使注意力投影矩阵以单独张量存储，也能实现高效正交化；(2) 与分布式优化器无缝集成，使优化器状态能够在数据并行 rank 间分片，同时保持正确的正交化语义；(3) 在 GPU 内存受限时，对 Muon 正交化缓冲进行 CPU 卸载。

MuonClip. Training trillion-parameter models introduces stability challenges where query-key dot products can grow unbounded, causing attention explosions [ 100 ] . MuonClip addresses this with hardware-accelerated implementations in cuDNN, cudnn-frontend, and Transformer Engine.

MuonClip。训练万亿参数模型带来了稳定性挑战，其中查询-键点积可能无界增长，导致注意力爆炸 [ 100 ]。MuonClip 通过 cuDNN、cudnn-frontend 与 Transformer Engine 中的硬件加速实现来解决这一问题。

8. Performance Evaluation（8. 性能评估）

The preceding sections described Megatron-Core MoE’s architecture, parallelism strategies, and optimizations. This section validates these contributions through empirical evaluation, showing the performance of the Megatron-Core MoE stack across diverse model configurations and hardware platforms.

前面的章节描述了 Megatron-Core 混合专家的架构、并行策略与优化。本节通过实证评估验证这些贡献，展示 Megatron-Core 混合专家技术栈在不同模型配置与硬件平台上的性能。

8.1. Experimental Setup（8.1. 实验设置）

Benchmark Models. We evaluate Megatron-Core MoE on two state-of-the-art fine-grained MoE architectures that stress-test the framework’s capabilities: DeepSeek-V3-685B [ 6 ] and Qwen3-235B [ 32 , 87 ] . Both models feature the fine-grained expert design that amplifies the Three Walls challenges: high expert counts strain memory capacity, top-k routing increases all-to-all communication volume, and smaller per-expert computations reduce GEMM efficiency. These characteristics make them demanding benchmarks for validating our optimization strategies.

基准模型。我们在两种最先进的细粒度混合专家架构上评估 Megatron-Core 混合专家，这些架构对框架能力进行了压力测试：DeepSeek-V3-685B [ 6 ] 与 Qwen3-235B [ 32 , 87 ]。两个模型都具有细粒度专家设计，放大了三堵墙的挑战：高专家数量给内存容量带来压力，top-k 路由增加了全互联通信量，较小的每专家计算降低了 GEMM 效率。这些特性使它们成为验证我们优化策略的苛刻基准。

Hardware and Software Stack. Benchmarks are conducted on NVIDIA GB200 and H100 GPUs to assess performance across hardware generations. We use the dev branch of Megatron-LM 5 5 5 https://github.com/NVIDIA/Megatron-LM/tree/dev with the latest TransformerEngine 6 6 6 https://github.com/NVIDIA/TransformerEngine/tree/main . The full optimization stack described in Section 4 is enabled, including but not limited to:

硬件与软件栈。基准测试在 NVIDIA GB200 与 H100 GPU 上进行，以评估跨硬件代际的性能。我们使用 Megatron-LM 的 dev 分支 5 5 5 https://github.com/NVIDIA/Megatron-LM/tree/dev 以及最新的 TransformerEngine 6 6 6 https://github.com/NVIDIA/TransformerEngine/tree/main。启用了第 4 节中描述的完整优化栈，包括但不限于：

FP8 training: MXFP8 on GB200/GB300 (Blackwell) with native Tensor Core support and blockwise FP8 on H100 (Hopper)

FP8 训练：GB200/GB300（Blackwell）上的 MXFP8，具有原生 Tensor Core 支持；H100（Hopper）上的块缩放（block scaling）FP8。

Memory optimizations: Selective recomputation and activation & optimizer state offloading to reduce peak memory footprint

内存优化：选择性重计算与激活值及优化器状态卸载，以降低峰值内存占用。

Optimized token dispatchers: HybridEP 7 7 7 https://github.com/deepseek-ai/DeepEP/tree/hybrid-ep on GB200/GB300 NVL systems exploiting Multi-Node NVLink, and DeepEP 8 8 8 https://github.com/deepseek-ai/DeepEP/tree/main on H100 systems

优化后的 token 分发器：GB200/GB300 NVL 系统上利用多节点 NVLink 的混合专家并行（HybridEP）7 7 7 https://github.com/deepseek-ai/DeepEP/tree/hybrid-ep，以及 H100 系统上的 DeepEP 8 8 8 https://github.com/deepseek-ai/DeepEP/tree/main。

Communication overlap: 1F1B all-to-all overlap with expert computation

通信重叠：全互联与专家计算的单前向单后向（1F1B）重叠。

Kernel optimizations: Grouped GEMM, router fusion, permutation fusion, and CUDA Graphs

内核优化：分组 GEMM、路由器融合、置换融合与 CUDA 图。

Metrics. We report per-GPU throughput in TFLOPS and tokens processed per second per GPU. These metrics provide two views of computational efficiency and practical training throughput.

指标。我们报告以 TFLOPS 计的每 GPU 吞吐与每 GPU 每秒处理的 token 数。这些指标提供了计算效率与实际训练吞吐的两个视角。

8.2. Key Performance Results（8.2. 关键性能结果）

Overview. Table 11 summarizes per-GPU throughput for representative fine-grained MoE training workloads on NVIDIA GB300, GB200, and H100 systems under the fully enabled optimization stack described in Section 4 . All configurations use force-balanced routing to ensure even token distribution across experts.

概览。表 11 总结了在第 4 节所述完全启用的优化栈下，NVIDIA GB300、GB200 与 H100 系统上代表性细粒度混合专家训练工作负载的每 GPU 吞吐。所有配置均使用强制均衡路由，以确保 token 在各专家间均匀分布。

DeepSeek-V3 and Qwen3-235B (large-scale training). Table 11 reports results for two contemporary, fine-grained MoE training workloads at a standard sequence length of 4,096, including a 1,024-GPU DeepSeek-V3 run on H100. Across these settings, the measurements demonstrate strong per-GPU efficiency and high sustained throughput across all three platforms.

DeepSeek-V3 与 Qwen3-235B（大规模训练）。表 11 报告了在 4,096 标准序列长度下，两个当代细粒度混合专家训练工作负载的结果，包括在 H100 上一次使用 1,024 块 GPU 的 DeepSeek-V3 运行。在这些设置下，测量结果表明三个平台都具有很强的每 GPU 效率与持续高吞吐。

FP8 Training Effectiveness. As shown in Table 11 , FP8 training achieves 368 TFLOPS per GPU on H100 for DeepSeek-V3, demonstrating that the blockwise FP8 recipe enables efficient training of large-scale MoE models on Hopper architecture. The optimizations discussed in Section 4.3.5 successfully address the challenges of applying FP8 to MoE models with dynamic expert routing and varying computation patterns, maintaining numerical stability while providing the performance benefits of reduced precision.

FP8 训练的有效性。如表 11 所示，FP8 训练在 H100 上为 DeepSeek-V3 实现了每 GPU 368 TFLOPS，表明块缩放 FP8 配方能够在 Hopper 架构上高效训练大规模混合专家模型。第 4.3.5 节中讨论的优化成功解决了将 FP8 应用于具有动态专家路由与多变计算模式的混合专家模型所面临的挑战，在保持数值稳定性的同时提供了降低精度带来的性能收益。

GB200 and GB300 Performance. The GB200 and GB300 platforms deliver approximately 3 $\times$ higher token throughput compared to H100 for both MoE models at comparable or smaller GPU counts. This efficiency gain stems from superior memory bandwidth, higher computational capacity, and native MXFP8 Tensor Core support. Combined with our software optimizations (Sections 4.1 and 4.3 ), GB200 achieves over 1,048 TFLOPS per GPU for DeepSeek-V3 training, and GB300 further pushes this to 1,233 TFLOPS per GPU.

GB200 与 GB300 的性能。对于两个混合专家模型，GB200 与 GB300 平台提供的 token 吞吐约为 H100 的 3 $\times$，且 GPU 数量相当或更少。这种效率提升源于更优的内存带宽、更高的计算能力与原生 MXFP8 Tensor Core 支持。结合我们的软件优化（第 4.1 节与第 4.3 节），GB200 为 DeepSeek-V3 训练实现了每 GPU 超过 1,048 TFLOPS，而 GB300 进一步将其推高至每 GPU 1,233 TFLOPS。

Scalability. These results demonstrate that Megatron-Core MoE scales efficiently to production workloads with thousands of GPUs and hundreds of billions of parameters. The successful deployment of DeepSeek-V3 at 1,024-GPU scale validates the effectiveness of parallel folding (Section 3.3 ), optimized dispatchers (Section 4.2 ), and kernel optimizations (Section 4.3 ).

可扩展性。这些结果表明，Megatron-Core 混合专家能够高效扩展到拥有数千块 GPU 与数千亿参数的生产工作负载。在 1,024 卡规模上成功部署 DeepSeek-V3，验证了并行折叠（Parallel Folding）（第 3.3 节）、优化后的分发器（第 4.2 节）与内核优化（第 4.3 节）的有效性。

Long-context Stress Test). To capture behavior beyond the standard 4,096-token regime, Table 11 includes a long-context Qwen3-235B run at sequence length 131,072 on GB300. Even in this memory- and communication-intensive setting, the system sustains 1,150 TFLOPS per GPU, indicating strong long-context efficiency under the full optimization stack.

长上下文压力测试）。为捕捉超出 4,096 token 标准区间的行为，表 11 包含了一次在 GB300 上、序列长度为 131,072 的长上下文 Qwen3-235B 运行。即使在这种内存与通信密集的场景下，系统仍维持每 GPU 1,150 TFLOPS，表明在完整优化栈下具有很强的长上下文效率。

Configuration Details. The parallelism configurations (TP, PP, CP, EP, DP, VPP, etc.), batch settings, and other optimization details used for each benchmark entry in Table 11 are provided in Appendix B . Note that these configurations represent our best-found settings through empirical tuning; they may not be globally optimal, as exhaustively searching the full parallelism and optimization configuration space is infeasible for models of this scale.

配置细节。表 11 中每个基准条目所用的并行配置（张量并行、流水线并行、上下文并行、专家并行、数据并行、虚拟流水线并行等）、批次设置与其他优化细节见附录 B。请注意，这些配置代表了我们通过实证调优找到的最佳设置；它们可能不是全局最优的，因为对于这种规模的模型，穷举搜索完整的并行与优化配置空间是不可行的。

Section 8 demonstrated that Megatron-Core MoE achieves state-of-the-art performance across diverse MoE architectures on the latest GPU platforms. We now examine the systematic methodology behind these results and show how the optimizations from Sections 4.1 – 4.3 translate into deployment decisions through detailed case studies.

第 8 节表明，Megatron-Core 混合专家在最新 GPU 平台上的多种混合专家架构中实现了最先进的性能。我们现在考察这些结果背后的系统方法论，并通过详细的案例研究展示第 4.1–4.3 节的优化如何转化为部署决策。

9. Performance Best Practices（9. 性能调优最佳实践）

Sections 3 – 7 presented a comprehensive optimization arsenal for MoE training: parallel folding, optimized dispatchers, Grouped GEMM, reduced-precision training, CUDA Graphs, and more. However, having many optimization techniques does not automatically translate into high performance . Each technique addresses specific bottlenecks but introduces its own trade-offs. Reduced-precision training reduces memory but requires careful precision management. Communication overlap improves throughput but adds memory overhead. CUDA Graphs eliminate host latency but conflict with dynamic tensor shapes. The challenge is not the availability of optimizations; it is knowing which optimizations to apply, when to apply them, and how they interact with each other.

第 3–7 节介绍了一套全面的混合专家（Mixture of Experts，MoE）训练优化方法：并行折叠（Parallel Folding）、优化后的分发器、分组 GEMM（Grouped GEMM）、低精度训练、CUDA 图（CUDA Graphs）等。然而，优化技术多并不自动意味着高性能。每种技术针对特定的瓶颈，但同时也带来自身的权衡。低精度训练减少了内存占用，但需要精细的精度管理。通信重叠提升了吞吐（throughput），但增加了内存开销。CUDA 图消除了宿主机延迟，但与动态张量形状冲突。挑战不在于优化技术是否可用，而在于知道该用哪些优化、何时使用，以及它们之间如何相互作用。

This complexity means that achieving peak performance requires a systematic methodology, not trial-and-error tuning. Through optimization of diverse MoE models (from Mixtral to DeepSeek-V3) on GB200 and H100, we developed a repeatable workflow for identifying bottlenecks and applying targeted solutions. This section distills those lessons into actionable guidance. Section 9.1 presents the methodology under the Three Walls framework with concrete examples for each phase. Section 9.2 then shows how these principles combine for DeepSeek-V3 on GB200 and H100, including why the same model requires different strategies on different hardware.

这种复杂性意味着，要达到峰值性能，需要系统化的方法论，而不是反复试错的调优。通过在 GB200 与 H100 上优化多种混合专家模型（从 Mixtral 到 DeepSeek-V3），我们总结出一套可复现的工作流，用于识别瓶颈并应用针对性的解决方案。本节将这些经验提炼为可操作的指导。第 9.1 节在三堵墙（Three Walls）框架下介绍该方法论，并为每个阶段给出具体示例。第 9.2 节则展示这些原则如何结合应用于 GB200 与 H100 上的 DeepSeek-V3，包括同一模型在不同硬件上为何需要不同策略。

9.1. A Systematic Optimization Workflow（9.1. 系统化优化工作流）

We present a three-phase workflow for MoE performance optimization. This methodology emerged from tuning Mixtral, DeepSeek-V3, and Qwen3 across GB200 and H100 platforms. The process is inherently iterative : solving one bottleneck often exposes the next, requiring continuous profiling and refinement.

我们提出一个三阶段工作流，用于混合专家性能优化。该方法论源于在 GB200 与 H100 平台上调优 Mixtral、DeepSeek-V3 与 Qwen3 的实践。这一过程本质上是迭代的：解决一个瓶颈往往会暴露下一个瓶颈，需要持续的性能分析与改进。

9.1.1. Phase 1: Establish Memory-Feasible Parallelism（9.1.1. 第一阶段：建立内存可行的并行配置）

Memory feasibility is the first constraint. Before optimizing for throughput, the configuration must fit in GPU memory. Table 12 summarizes how each parallelism strategy affects per-GPU memory and communication overhead.

内存可行性是第一个约束条件。在为吞吐优化之前，配置必须能装入 GPU 内存。表 12 总结了每种并行策略对每 GPU 内存与通信开销的影响。

表： Table 12 : Impact of parallelism strategies on memory and communication. $d$ = parallelism degree. † Requires distributed optimizer ( --use-distributed-optimizer ).

表 12：并行策略对内存与通信的影响。$d$ = 并行度。† 需要分布式优化器（--use-distributed-optimizer）。

策略 峰值激活值 权重内存 优化器状态 通信（每层）
TP $1/d$（使用 SP） $1/d$ $1/d$ 高
EP $\sim$ 1（依赖于负载） $1/d$（仅 MoE） $1/d$ 中
PP 1（使用 VPP 时 $>$ 1） $1/d$ $1/d$ 中
CP $1/d$ 1 $1/d^{\dagger}$ 中
DP 1 1 $1/d^{\dagger}$ 低

Example. For a 685B-parameter fine-grained MoE model, BF16 activations alone can exceed 130 GB per GPU (see Table 3 ), immediately ruling out baseline configurations on 80 GB devices even with parallelism, and motivating the memory optimizations in Phase 3.

示例。对于一个 685B 参数的细粒度混合专家模型，仅 BF16 激活值（activation）一项就可能超过每 GPU 130 GB（见表 3），即使使用并行也立即排除了在 80 GB 设备上采用基线配置的可能，从而凸显了第三阶段中内存优化的必要性。

9.1.2. Phase 2: Select Optimal Parallelism Strategy（9.1.2. 第二阶段：选择最优并行策略）

Once memory-feasible configurations are identified, select the strategy that minimizes communication overhead while maintaining throughput. The optimal choice depends on model architecture, sequence length, and hardware topology.

在确定内存可行的配置之后，选择能在保持吞吐的同时最小化通信开销的策略。最优选择取决于模型架构、序列长度与硬件拓扑。

Goal: Keep TP/EP/PP/CP as small as possible while avoiding OOM.

目标：将张量并行（TP）/专家并行（EP）/流水线并行（PP）/上下文并行（CP）保持在尽可能小的规模，同时避免内存溢出（OOM）。

Why: Model parallelism introduces communication overhead that hurts performance.

原因：模型并行会引入损害性能的通信开销。

How: Use distributed optimizer ( --use-distributed-optimizer ) to shard optimizer states across DP ranks, freeing memory for larger DP size.

方法：使用分布式优化器（--use-distributed-optimizer）将优化器状态分片到各个数据并行（DP）rank 上，释放内存以支持更大的数据并行规模。

Goal: Ensure EP $\times$ TP fits within the NVLink Domain (typically 8 GPUs in a single node unless Multi-Node NVLink is used).

目标：确保专家并行 × 张量并行适配 NVLink 域（通常为单个节点内的 8 块 GPU，除非使用多节点 NVLink）。

Why: EP and TP are communication-intensive; NVLink provides much higher bandwidth than cross-node interconnects.

原因：专家并行与张量并行都是通信密集型的；NVLink 提供的带宽远高于跨节点互连。

Scaling: When scaling beyond the NVLink Domain, prefer PP over expanding TP/EP across nodes.

扩展：当扩展超出 NVLink 域时，优先使用流水线并行，而不是跨节点扩展张量并行/专家并行。

Note: For very large MoE models like DeepSeek-V3, EP communication volume may exceed what NVLink can hide. In this case, enable communication-computation overlap (see Section 4.2 ) to hide EP latency behind computation.

注意：对于 DeepSeek-V3 这样的大规模混合专家模型，专家并行通信量可能超过 NVLink 能够隐藏的范围。在这种情况下，启用通信-计算重叠（见第 4.2 节），将专家并行延迟隐藏在计算之后。

Goal: Use PP to distribute layers across nodes while keeping EP $\times$ TP within NVLink.

目标：使用流水线并行将各层分布到多个节点，同时将专家并行 × 张量并行保持在 NVLink 域内。

VPP: Enable Virtual Pipeline Parallelism to reduce pipeline bubbles when PP $\geq 2$ .

虚拟流水线并行：当流水线并行 $\geq$ 2 时，启用虚拟流水线并行（Virtual Pipeline Parallelism，VPP）以减少流水线气泡（pipeline bubble）。

Config: Set --pipeline-model-parallel-layout to control Pipeline Parallelism settings (see Section 7.5 ). Larger VPP usually reduces pipeline bubbles but increases P2P communications; a middle value often provides the best balance. The workloads of each VPP rank should be balanced to maximize the throughput.

配置：设置 --pipeline-model-parallel-layout 以控制流水线并行设置（见第 7.5 节）。较大的虚拟流水线并行通常能减少流水线气泡，但会增加点对点通信；中间值往往能取得最佳平衡。每个虚拟流水线并行 rank 的工作负载应当均衡，以最大化吞吐。

EP offers several advantages over TP for MoE layers:

专家并行在混合专家层上相比张量并行有以下几项优势：

Better GEMM efficiency: Larger local matrix sizes improve GPU utilization.

更高的 GEMM 效率：更大的本地矩阵尺寸可提高 GPU 利用率。

Lower communication: EP has less communication overhead than TP for MoE layers.

更低的通信量：对于混合专家层，专家并行的通信开销小于张量并行。

Simpler computation graph: Easier to overlap communication with computation.

更简单的计算图：更容易将通信与计算重叠。

Token permutation: When EP $=$ num_experts , local token permutation is eliminated.

token 置换：当专家并行 $=$ 专家数量时，可消除本地 token 置换。

Example: For Mixtral-8 $\times$ 7B, EP8 $\times$ TP1 outperforms EP4 $\times$ TP2.

示例：对于 Mixtral-8×7B，EP8 × TP1 的性能优于 EP4 × TP2。

When to use: Sequence length $\geq$ 8K tokens.

适用场景：序列长度 $\geq$ 8K token 时。

When to avoid: For sequences $<$ 4K tokens, CP overhead typically exceeds benefits.

避免使用的场景：对于序列长度 $<$ 4K token 的情况，上下文并行的开销通常超过其收益。

Key factor: CP efficiency depends on overlapping communication with computation.

关键因素：上下文并行的效率取决于能否将通信与计算重叠。

Config: Set --context-parallel-size to partition sequences across GPUs.

配置：设置 --context-parallel-size，将序列划分到多个 GPU 上。

Example. Consider a 256-expert MoE model on an NVL72 system. Applying Guideline 4, Parallel Folding sets expert TP = 1 so that each expert runs on a single GPU, maximizing GEMM efficiency. With Guideline 2, EP64 fits entirely within the NVLink domain. Guideline 1 then drives the remaining choices: the available memory budget determines TP and PP for attention layers, with DP filling the rest.

示例。考虑一个在 NVL72 系统上的 256 专家混合专家模型。应用指南 4，并行折叠将专家张量并行设为 1，使每个专家（expert）在单个 GPU 上运行，从而最大化 GEMM 效率。根据指南 2，EP64 完全适配 NVLink 域。指南 1 则决定其余选择：可用的内存预算决定了注意力层的张量并行与流水线并行，数据并行填充剩余部分。

9.1.3. Phase 3: Profile and Optimize Bottlenecks（9.1.3. 第三阶段：性能分析与瓶颈优化）

With a working parallelism configuration established, profile the training run to identify which wall dominates. Apply targeted optimizations based on the bottleneck type.

在建立可行的并行配置之后，对训练过程进行性能分析，以确定哪堵墙占主导。根据瓶颈类型应用针对性的优化。

Symptom: Forced to use full recomputation or excessively large parallelism degrees to avoid OOM.

症状：为避免内存溢出，被迫使用全量重计算（recomputation）或过大的并行度。

Solutions: Apply memory-saving techniques (Table 13 ) to reduce memory consumption.

解决方案：应用节省内存的技术（表 13），降低内存消耗。

表： Table 13 : Memory bottleneck solutions.

表 13：内存瓶颈解决方案。

症状：性能分析显示大量时间消耗在集合通信操作上。

解决方案：确定哪种通信是瓶颈，并应用相应的优化（表 14）。

The Compute Efficiency Wall manifests in two distinct forms: CPU overhead and computation inefficiency. CPU overhead occurs when the host cannot launch GPU kernels fast enough, creating gaps in GPU execution.

计算效率墙（Compute Efficiency Wall）表现为两种不同形式：CPU 开销与计算低效。当宿主机无法足够快地启动 GPU 内核时，就会产生 CPU 开销，在 GPU 执行中造成空隙。

Symptom: Nsight Systems timeline shows gaps between GPU kernels where CPU cannot launch kernels fast enough.

症状：Nsight Systems 时间线显示 GPU 内核之间存在空隙，原因是 CPU 无法足够快地启动内核。

Solutions: Reduce host-side overhead by minimizing kernel launches and enabling CUDA Graphs (Table 15 ).

解决方案：通过减少内核启动次数并启用 CUDA 图来降低宿主机端开销（表 15）。

表： Table 15 : CPU overhead bottleneck solutions.

表 15：CPU 开销瓶颈解决方案。

优化 配置 参考
禁用 Python GC --manual-gc --manual-gc-interval 10 —
减少内核启动 减小 TP 或增大 MBS —
启用 CUDA 图 --cuda-graph-impl transformer_engine § 4.3.6

Computation inefficiency occurs when GPU kernels themselves underutilize hardware resources, typically due to small GEMM sizes in fine-grained MoE architectures.

当 GPU 内核本身未能充分利用硬件资源时，就会出现计算低效，细粒度混合专家架构中 GEMM 尺寸较小通常是其原因。

Symptom: GPU SM utilization is low despite no communication or CPU bottlenecks.

症状：尽管不存在通信或 CPU 瓶颈，GPU SM 利用率仍然很低。

Solutions: Improve kernel efficiency through batching, fusion, and lower precision (Table 16 ).

解决方案：通过批处理、融合与更低精度来提高内核效率（表 16）。

表： Table 16 : Computation bottleneck solutions.

表 16：计算瓶颈解决方案。

优化 配置 参考
分组 GEMM --moe-grouped-gemm § 4.3.2
内核融合 --moe-router-fusion --moe-permute-fusion § 4.3.2
FP8 精度 --fp8-format --fp8-recipe § 5

Example. On an NVL8 system where EP spans across nodes, profiling may reveal all-to-all communication consuming 30–50% of step time, pointing squarely at the Communication Wall. On an NVL72 system where the same EP degree stays within the NVLink domain, the dominant bottleneck after enabling FP8 often shifts to CPU overhead instead; the faster GPU computation exposes host-side launch latency. The same model on different hardware can require entirely different optimization strategies.

示例。在一个 NVL8 系统上，当专家并行跨节点时，性能分析可能显示全互联（all-to-all）通信占用了 30–50% 的 step 时间，明确指向通信墙。在一个 NVL72 系统上，当同样的专家并行度保持在 NVLink 域内时，在启用 FP8 之后，主要瓶颈往往转为 CPU 开销；更快的 GPU 计算暴露了宿主机端的启动延迟。同一模型在不同硬件上可能需要完全不同的优化策略。

9.1.4. Summary（9.1.4. 小结）

The ordering of the three phases matters: memory constraints are a hard barrier that must be resolved first (Phase 1), parallelism decisions determine the communication topology (Phase 2), and only then can profiling reveal which wall to attack (Phase 3). Crucially, this process is iterative . Memory optimizations may enable smaller parallelism degrees, returning to Phase 1. Some Phase 3 optimizations have their own memory cost (EP communication overlap requires extra buffers, CUDA Graphs consume additional memory), which may require revisiting earlier decisions. Continuous profiling after each round guides the next iteration.

三个阶段的顺序很重要：内存约束是必须首先解决的硬性障碍（第一阶段），并行决策决定了通信拓扑（第二阶段），只有在此之后，性能分析才能揭示应攻击哪堵墙（第三阶段）。关键在于，这一过程是迭代的。内存优化可能使更小的并行度成为可能，从而回到第一阶段。某些第三阶段优化本身也有内存成本（专家并行通信重叠需要额外的缓冲区，CUDA 图会消耗额外内存），这可能需要重新审视早期的决策。每轮之后持续的性能分析将指导下一轮迭代。

9.2. Case Study: Tuning DeepSeek-V3 on GB200 and H100（9.2. 案例分析：在 GB200 与 H100 上调优 DeepSeek-V3）

DeepSeek-V3 represents an extreme stress test for MoE training: 685B total parameters with Multi-Token Prediction (MTP) and Multi-Latent Attention (MLA). All three walls apply: memory pressure from 256 experts, high all-to-all volume from top-8 routing, and small per-expert GEMMs. The preceding workflow (Section 9.1 ) showed how to approach each phase individually; this case study examines how those decisions interact to form a coherent optimization stack, and why the same model requires fundamentally different strategies on GB200 and H100.

DeepSeek-V3 是混合专家训练的一项极端压力测试：685B 总参数，带多 token 预测（Multi-Token Prediction，MTP）与多头隐式注意力（Multi-Latent Attention，MLA）。三堵墙全部适用：256 个专家带来的内存压力、top-8 路由带来的大量全互联通信量，以及较小的每专家 GEMM。上一节的工作流（第 9.1 节）展示了如何逐个阶段处理这些问题；本案例分析则考察这些决策如何相互作用，形成一套连贯的优化组合，以及同一模型在 GB200 与 H100 上为何需要根本不同的策略。

9.2.1. Final Optimized Configuration and Performance（9.2.1. 最终优化配置与性能）

9.2.2. Anatomy of the Optimized Configuration（9.2.2. 优化配置解析）

Both platforms use Parallel Folding (Guideline 4) to decouple attention and expert parallelism: TP applies only to attention layers for memory reduction, while experts use EP with TP = 1 for better GEMM efficiency. EP64 is chosen so that each GPU holds exactly four of the 256 experts, eliminating local token permutation overhead.

两个平台都使用并行折叠（指南 4）来解耦注意力与专家并行：张量并行仅适用于注意力层以减少内存，而专家使用专家并行且专家张量并行 = 1，以获得更好的 GEMM 效率。选择 EP64 是为了让每块 GPU 恰好持有 256 个专家中的 4 个，从而消除本地 token 置换开销。

The key platform difference is memory capacity. GB200’s 192 GB per GPU (vs. H100’s 80 GB) allows TP1/PP4 instead of TP2/PP8, halving the pipeline depth, which reduces pipeline bubbles and simplifies workload balancing (Guideline 1). Flexible VPP then fine-tunes the pipeline: on H100, the layout Et3|(tt|)29m|L groups embedding with 3 transformer layers in the first stage, places MTP in a standalone stage, and separates the loss; on GB200, Et4|(tttt|)14tmL distributes layers across 16 virtual stages for balanced computation across the shorter pipeline. These parallelism configurations establish the foundation for all subsequent optimizations.

关键的平台差异在于内存容量。GB200 每 GPU 192 GB（而 H100 为 80 GB），因此可以使用 TP1/PP4 而不是 TP2/PP8，使流水线深度减半，从而减少流水线气泡并简化工作负载均衡（指南 1）。灵活的虚拟流水线并行随后对流水线进行微调：在 H100 上，布局 Et3|(tt|)29m|L 将 embedding 与 3 个 transformer 层放在第一阶段，将多 token 预测放在独立阶段，并分离损失；在 GB200 上，Et4|(tttt|)14tmL 将各层分布到 16 个虚拟阶段，使较短流水线上的计算保持均衡。这些并行配置为后续所有优化奠定了基础。

FP8 training is the first lever: it halves activation memory on both platforms, freeing GPU memory that would otherwise require aggressive recomputation. On H100, the freed memory budget is critical because it enables EP communication overlap, which requires extra buffers for pipelining dispatch and combine operations (see Communication Wall below). Fine-grained recomputation ( mlp , mla_up_proj , layernorm , moe_act ) trims remaining memory pressure. Memory-efficient permutation eliminates redundant activation storage, and low-precision optimizer states (BF16) provide additional headroom.

FP8 训练是第一个杠杆：它使两个平台上的激活内存减半，释放了原本需要激进重计算的 GPU 内存。在 H100 上，释放出的内存预算至关重要，因为它使专家并行通信重叠成为可能——重叠需要额外的缓冲区，用于对分发（dispatch）与合并（combine）操作进行流水线化（见下文通信墙（Communication Wall））。细粒度重计算（mlp、mla_up_proj、layernorm、moe_act）削减了剩余的内存压力。内存高效置换消除了冗余的激活存储，低精度优化器状态（BF16）提供了额外的余量。

On GB200, the memory chain resolves differently. NVL72 keeps EP local, so communication overlap is not critical and its memory budget is freed entirely. GB200’s higher C2C bandwidth (NVLink-C2C) makes optimizer state offloading effective: the transfer overhead is low enough that offloading frees substantial GPU memory for activations. This in turn reduces the need for fine-grained recomputation to just mlp , far less aggressive than on H100.

在 GB200 上，内存链条的解决方式不同。NVL72 使专家并行保持本地，因此通信重叠并不关键，其内存预算被完全释放。GB200 更高的 C2C 带宽（NVLink-C2C）使优化器状态卸载（offloading）变得有效：传输开销足够低，卸载可以为激活释放大量 GPU 内存。这反过来又将细粒度重计算的需求降低到仅 mlp，远不如 H100 上那么激进。

On H100/B200 (NVL8), EP64 spans 8 nodes, and cross-node all-to-all latency would consume nearly 50% of step time with the standard all-to-all dispatcher. DeepEP reduces this overhead through fused permutation and optimized collective operations. Even so, EP communication overlap is essential to hide the remaining cross-node latency behind expert computation, and this overlap is only feasible because FP8 freed enough memory for the extra buffers (see Memory Wall above).

在 H100/B200（NVL8）上，EP64 横跨 8 个节点，跨节点全互联延迟在使用标准全互联分发器（dispatcher）时将占用近 50% 的 step 时间。DeepEP 通过融合置换与优化的集合操作降低了这一开销。即便如此，专家并行通信重叠对于隐藏剩余的跨节点延迟仍然必不可少，而这种重叠之所以可行，恰恰是因为 FP8 释放了足够的内存来容纳额外的缓冲区（见上文内存墙（Memory Wall））。

On GB200 (NVL72), EP64 stays entirely within the NVLink domain. HybridEP fully utilizes the 1.8 TB/s bidirectional bandwidth without requiring communication overlap. The communication wall is effectively resolved by hardware topology alone, shifting the dominant bottleneck to compute efficiency.

在 GB200（NVL72）上，EP64 完全保持在 NVLink 域内。混合专家并行充分利用了 1.8 TB/s 的双向带宽，无需通信重叠。通信墙实际上仅由硬件拓扑就得到了解决，使主要瓶颈转移到了计算效率。

FP8 accelerates GEMMs on both platforms (blockwise FP8 on H100, MXFP8 with native Blackwell Tensor Core support on GB200), but this speedup has a side effect: faster GPU computation exposes CPU overhead. On GB200, where NVL72 already eliminates the communication bottleneck, CPU overhead becomes the dominant constraint; the host cannot launch kernels fast enough to keep the GPU saturated.

FP8 在两个平台上都加速了 GEMM（H100 上为分块 FP8，GB200 上为带原生 Blackwell Tensor Core 支持的 MXFP8），但这种加速有一个副作用：更快的 GPU 计算暴露了 CPU 开销。在 GB200 上，NVL72 已经消除了通信瓶颈，CPU 开销成为主要约束；宿主机无法足够快地启动内核，使 GPU 保持饱和。

Partial CUDA Graphs address this by capturing attention, router, and MoE preprocessing into static graphs, while leaving dynamic expert computation ungraphed. Kernel fusions (router fusion, permute fusion, MLA RoPE fusion) reduce kernel launch count. CPU/NUMA binding 10 10 10 The bindpcie script at https://github.com/NVIDIA/mlperf-common/blob/main/client/bindpcie automatically detects GPU/NUMA topology based on local rank and uses numactl to bind each process’s CPU and memory to the NUMA node nearest its GPU. reduces host-side memory access latency.

部分 CUDA 图通过将注意力、路由器（router）与混合专家预处理捕获到静态图中来解决这一问题，同时将动态专家计算保留在图外。内核融合（kernel fusion，包括路由器融合、置换融合与 MLA RoPE 融合）减少了内核启动次数。CPU/NUMA 绑定——bindpcie 脚本（https://github.com/NVIDIA/mlperf-common/blob/main/client/bindpcie）可根据本地 rank 自动检测 GPU/NUMA 拓扑，并使用 numactl 将每个进程的 CPU 与内存绑定到离其 GPU 最近的 NUMA 节点——可降低宿主机端内存访问延迟。

The cross-cutting nature of these optimizations is evident throughout: FP8 simultaneously reduces memory (activations halved), and improves compute throughput, but introduces quantization kernels that amplify CPU overhead. The same model arrives at fundamentally different optimization stacks on different hardware: H100’s stack centers on hiding communication latency (DeepEP + EP overlap), while GB200’s centers on eliminating CPU overhead (CUDA Graphs + kernel fusions). This contrast validates the profile-driven iterative approach from Section 9.1 .

这些优化的跨领域特性贯穿始终：FP8 同时减少了内存（激活减半）并提升了计算吞吐，但引入的量化内核加剧了 CPU 开销。同一模型在不同硬件上形成了根本不同的优化组合：H100 的组合以隐藏通信延迟为核心（DeepEP + 专家并行重叠），而 GB200 的组合以消除 CPU 开销为核心（CUDA 图 + 内核融合）。这种对比验证了第 9.1 节中基于性能分析的迭代方法。

9.2.3. Lessons Learned（9.2.3. 经验教训）

Four insights generalize beyond DeepSeek-V3:

以下四点经验可推广到 DeepSeek-V3 之外：

Platform characteristics drive strategy : GB200’s larger memory (192GB vs 80GB) and higher C2C bandwidth enable more aggressive choices: smaller PP degree, less fine-grained recomputation, and effective optimizer offloading. H100’s NVL8 requires EP communication overlap to hide cross-node latency, while GB200’s NVL72 keeps EP64 within the NVLink domain.

平台特性决定策略：GB200 更大的内存（192GB 对比 80GB）与更高的 C2C 带宽使更激进的选择成为可能：更小的流水线并行度、粒度更粗的重计算，以及有效的优化器卸载。H100 的 NVL8 需要专家并行通信重叠来隐藏跨节点延迟，而 GB200 的 NVL72 使 EP64 保持在 NVLink 域内。

Parallel Folding unlocks flexibility : Decoupling attention TP from expert EP allows independent optimization of each layer type. Combined with flexible VPP, this enables fine-grained workload balancing across pipeline stages.

并行折叠带来灵活性：将注意力层的张量并行与专家层的专家并行解耦，使每种层类型都可以独立优化。结合灵活的虚拟流水线并行，这使流水线各阶段之间可以实现细粒度的工作负载均衡。

FP8 shifts bottlenecks : FP8 training accelerates GEMMs and reduces memory, but amplifies CPU overhead as the dominant bottleneck. CUDA Graphs, kernel fusions, and CPU/NUMA binding become essential.

FP8 转移瓶颈：FP8 训练加速了 GEMM 并减少了内存，但使 CPU 开销成为主要瓶颈。CUDA 图、内核融合与 CPU/NUMA 绑定变得必不可少。

Iterative optimization : The optimization process is inherently cyclical. Memory optimizations (recomputation, offloading) free GPU memory, enabling communication overlap. Communication optimizations expose compute efficiency bottlenecks. Some optimizations have cross-cutting effects: FP8 training reduces both memory and compute time but increases CPU overhead; CUDA Graphs reduce CPU overhead but consume additional memory. These interactions mean that applying one optimization may require revisiting earlier decisions. Continuous profiling after each change guides the next optimization target and helps identify when diminishing returns suggest moving to the next bottleneck.

迭代优化：优化过程本质上是循环的。内存优化（重计算、卸载）释放 GPU 内存，使通信重叠成为可能。通信优化暴露了计算效率瓶颈。某些优化具有跨领域效应：FP8 训练同时减少内存与计算时间，但增加了 CPU 开销；CUDA 图减少了 CPU 开销，但消耗额外内存。这些相互作用意味着，应用一项优化可能需要重新审视早期的决策。每次变更后持续的性能分析可以指导下一个优化目标，并帮助识别何时收益递减、应转向下一个瓶颈。

10. Megatron-Core MoE in Reinforcement Learning（10. 强化学习中的 Megatron-Core MoE）

Reinforcement learning (RL) post-training has become a critical paradigm following OpenAI o1 and DeepSeek-R1 [ 81 ] . Many leading RL-trained models are MoE architectures (DeepSeek-R1, Kimi-K2, etc.), and RL workloads introduce challenges that amplify the MoE-specific issues discussed throughout this report: highly variable sequence lengths stress the Memory Wall, interleaved inference and training phases demand rapid memory offloading, and routing discrepancies between inference and training engines threaten stability. To achieve high training throughput at scale, RL frameworks run Megatron-Core inside Ray workers as the distributed training backend, and use Megatron-Bridge for bidirectional Hugging Face to Megatron checkpoint conversion.

强化学习（Reinforcement Learning，RL）后训练已成为继 OpenAI o1 与 DeepSeek-R1 之后的关键范式 [ 81 ]。许多领先的强化学习训练模型采用混合专家架构（DeepSeek-R1、Kimi-K2 等），而强化学习工作负载带来的挑战放大了本报告中讨论的混合专家特有问题：高度可变的序列长度给内存墙带来压力，推理与训练阶段交错进行要求快速的内存卸载，而推理与训练引擎之间的路由差异威胁稳定性。为了实现大规模高训练吞吐，强化学习框架在 Ray worker 内运行 Megatron-Core 作为分布式训练后端，并使用 Megatron-Bridge 在 Hugging Face 与 Megatron 检查点（checkpoint）格式之间进行双向转换。

10.1. Challenges for RL Post-Training（10.1. 强化学习后训练的挑战）

RL post-training differs from pre-training in ways that impose new requirements on the training engine:

强化学习后训练与预训练的不同之处在于，它对训练引擎提出了新的要求：

Variable-length sequences. Pre-training typically operates on fixed-length sequences (e.g., 4K tokens). RL workloads, by contrast, produce highly variable sequence lengths: the maximum may reach 128K or even 1M tokens, while the mean within a mini-batch is often one-half to one-quarter of the maximum. This long-tailed distribution makes it difficult to balance compute efficiency against peak memory consumption.

可变长度序列。预训练通常使用固定长度的序列（例如 4K token）。相比之下，强化学习工作负载产生的序列长度高度可变：最大值可达 128K 甚至 1M token，而一个 mini-batch 内的均值往往只有最大值的一半到四分之一。这种长尾分布使计算效率与峰值内存消耗难以平衡。

Memory offloading. RL frameworks typically co-locate a training engine and an inference engine on the same GPUs, offloading one engine’s state while the other is active. This requires both engines to release and restore their full memory footprint quickly and completely—a non-trivial requirement when optimizer states, activations, and KV caches must all be managed.

内存卸载。强化学习框架通常在同一块 GPU 上同时部署训练引擎与推理引擎，在其中一个引擎工作时卸载另一个引擎的状态。这要求两个引擎都能快速、完整地释放并恢复其全部内存占用——当优化器状态、激活值与 KV 缓存（KV cache）都需要管理时，这并非易事。

Online weight export. The inference engine must be updated with the latest parameters after each training step. This requires the training engine to export weights rapidly in a format the inference engine can load, across diverse model architectures.

在线权重导出。推理引擎必须在每个训练 step 之后用最新参数更新。这要求训练引擎以推理引擎可加载的格式快速导出权重，并跨越不同的模型架构。

Training stability. Standard RL algorithms assume that sampled responses come from the current policy distribution. In practice, however, the inference and training engines use different optimized kernels, producing slightly different token probabilities even with identical parameters—effectively introducing off-policy bias. For MoE models, this problem is compounded: tokens from the same sequence may be routed to different experts in the inference and training engines, amplifying the discrepancy.

训练稳定性。标准强化学习算法假设采样得到的响应来自当前策略分布。然而在实践中，推理与训练引擎使用不同的优化内核，即使参数相同，也会产生略有不同的 token 概率——实际上引入了 off-policy 偏差。对于混合专家模型，这一问题更加复杂：同一序列中的 token 可能在推理与训练引擎中被路由到不同的专家，从而放大差异。

10.2. Megatron-Bridge（10.2. Megatron-Bridge）

A typical RL workflow starts from a pretrained Hugging Face model and fine-tunes it in an RL framework. At scale, these frameworks often run Megatron-Core inside Ray workers for distributed training, while rollout commonly uses an inference stack that expects Hugging Face-format checkpoints. This creates two integration needs: mapping model definitions to Megatron-Core modules, and converting checkpoints between Hugging Face and Megatron formats efficiently. Megatron-Bridge addresses the checkpoint interoperability layer, enabling fast HF-to-Megatron conversion for initialization, training, and export. This pattern is used across multiple RL frameworks, including veRL [ 102 ] , Slime [ 103 ] , and NeMo RL.

典型的强化学习工作流从一个预训练的 Hugging Face 模型开始，在强化学习框架中对其进行微调。在大规模场景下，这些框架通常在 Ray worker 内运行 Megatron-Core 进行分布式训练，而 rollout 通常使用期望 Hugging Face 格式检查点的推理栈。这产生了两项集成需求：将模型定义映射到 Megatron-Core 模块，以及在 Hugging Face 与 Megatron 格式之间高效转换检查点。Megatron-Bridge 解决了检查点互操作层，支持快速的 HF 到 Megatron 转换，用于初始化、训练与导出。这一模式已在多个强化学习框架中使用，包括 veRL [ 102 ]、Slime [ 103 ] 与 NeMo RL。

10.3. Megatron-Core Optimization for Reinforcement Learning（10.3. 面向强化学习的 Megatron-Core 优化）

Packed Sequence Support. As discussed in Section 6.4.1 , we can pack sequences to remove the padding from a batch of variable sequence lengths. Building on packed sequence support, on the RL framework side, we recommend using a packing-aware dynamic batch size, which ensures that every batch has a similar total number of effective tokens. Moreover, we introduce an additional load–balancing strategy that explicitly accounts for the heterogeneous computational cost of the transformer blocks. Because the attention module scales quadratically with sequence length ( $O(L^{2})$ ) whereas the feed-forward network (FFN) scales linearly ( $O(L)$ ), a batch that is well balanced in terms of token count can still be highly unbalanced in wall-clock time. To mitigate this issue, we first compute, for every sample in the batch, the square of its sequence length and take the sum within the mini-batch. We then sort micro-batches according to this “attention cost” metric and schedule them in a small-to-large-to-small pattern (i.e., increasing order followed by decreasing order). This serpentine ordering delivers two benefits. (i) For data parallelism, pipeline parallelism, and expert parallelism, it reduces synchronization bubbles because consecutive micro-batches have comparable attention workloads. (ii) Within pipeline parallelism, the warm-up and cool-down phases, where stages are traditionally under-utilized, observe shorter idle periods, as lighter micro-batches arrive earlier and later in the schedule.

打包序列支持。如第 6.4.1 节所述，我们可以将序列打包，以消除一批可变序列长度中的填充。在打包序列支持的基础上，在强化学习框架方面，我们建议使用考虑打包的动态 batch size，确保每个 batch 具有相似的有效 token 总数。此外，我们还引入了一种额外的负载均衡（load balancing）策略，该策略明确考虑了 transformer block 的异构计算成本。由于注意力模块随序列长度呈二次方扩展（$O(L^{2})$），而前馈网络（Feed-Forward Network，FFN）呈线性扩展（$O(L)$），一个按 token 数量均衡的 batch 在 wall-clock 时间上仍可能高度不均衡。为缓解这一问题，我们首先为 batch 中的每个样本计算其序列长度的平方，并取 mini-batch 内的总和。然后，我们按照这种“注意力成本”度量对微批次（micro-batch）排序，并以小到大再到小的模式（即先升序后降序）进行调度。这种蛇形排序带来两点好处。（i）对于数据并行、流水线并行与专家并行，它减少了同步气泡，因为相邻微批次具有相当的注意力工作负载。（ii）在流水线并行中，传统上利用率不足的预热与冷却阶段空闲时间更短，因为较轻的微批次在调度中较早和较晚到达。

Dynamic Context Parallelism. As discussed in Section 6.4.2 , conventional training setups must pick a fixed context-parallel (CP) degree that guarantees the longest sequence in the workload will not trigger out-of-memory (OOM) failures. This conservative choice forces the majority of shorter sequences, whose memory footprints are far smaller, to run with an unnecessarily large CP degree, thereby wasting cross-device bandwidth and reducing overall throughput. Dynamic CP removes this one-size-fits-all restriction by selecting the CP degree adaptively on a per-micro-batch basis. At runtime we bucket incoming sequences by length, choose the minimal CP degree that keeps each bucket within the device memory budget, and dispatch micro-batches accordingly. Long sequences receive a higher CP degree to stay memory-safe, whereas short sequences are executed with a lower CP degree that maximizes arithmetic intensity and reduces communication volume.

动态上下文并行。如第 6.4.2 节所述，传统的训练设置必须选择一个固定的上下文并行度，以保证工作负载中最长的序列不会触发内存溢出故障。这种保守的选择迫使大多数较短的序列——其内存占用要小得多——不得不以不必要的大上下文并行度运行，从而浪费跨设备带宽并降低整体吞吐。动态上下文并行消除了这种一刀切的限制，它在每个微批次的基础上自适应地选择上下文并行度。在运行时，我们按长度对输入序列分桶，选择能使每个桶保持在设备内存预算内的最小上下文并行度，并相应地分发微批次。长序列获得更高的上下文并行度以保持内存安全，而短序列则以更低的上下文并行度执行，从而最大化算术强度并减少通信量。

CPU Optimizer Offloading. To further relieve GPU memory pressure, we offload the optimizer states to host DRAM during the forward and backward passes, loading them back onto the GPU only for the parameter-update step. Because optimizer tensors can occupy multiple times the size of model parameters, temporarily evicting them frees a substantial amount of high-bandwidth GPU memory that can instead be used to cache activations or accommodate longer sequences.

CPU 优化器卸载。为进一步缓解 GPU 内存压力，我们在前向与反向传播期间将优化器状态卸载到宿主机 DRAM，仅在参数更新步骤时将其加载回 GPU。由于优化器张量的体积可能是模型参数的数倍，暂时将其移出可释放大量高带宽 GPU 内存，这些内存可用于缓存激活或容纳更长的序列。

FP16 Training Support. Although bfloat16 (BF16) is widely adopted for pre-training large language models, several studies have observed that, during reinforcement-learning training, half-precision floating-point (FP16) can deliver greater numerical stability under certain hyper-parameter choices. Megatron-Core MoE implementation therefore offers a fully-featured FP16 path, including loss scaling and mixed-precision optimizer kernels, enabling practitioners to select the precision mode that best aligns with their stability requirements without sacrificing throughput.

FP16 训练支持。尽管 bfloat16（BF16）已被广泛用于预训练大语言模型，但多项研究观察到，在强化学习训练期间，半精度浮点数（FP16）在某些超参数选择下可提供更高的数值稳定性。因此，Megatron-Core 的混合专家实现提供了一条功能完备的 FP16 路径，包括损失缩放与混合精度优化器内核，使实践者能够选择最符合其稳定性要求的精度模式，而无需牺牲吞吐。

Router Replay. Recent work [ 104 ] shows that capturing the routing decisions produced by the MoE router during inference and replaying them in subsequent training phases can improve convergence consistency. Thanks to a community-contributed patch, Megatron-Core MoE now supports this feature: the inference engine logs each token’s expert assignment, and the training stack can ingest and enforce the same routing pattern. This mechanism decouples routing variability from weight updates, leading to more stable optimization trajectories, especially in RL settings where on-policy data distribution shifts are frequent.

路由器重放。最近的研究 [ 104 ] 表明，捕获推理期间混合专家路由器产生的路由决策，并在随后的训练阶段重放这些决策，可以提高收敛一致性。得益于社区贡献的补丁，Megatron-Core 的混合专家现在支持这一功能：推理引擎记录每个 token 的专家分配，训练栈可以摄取并强制执行相同的路由模式。这一机制将路由可变性与权重更新解耦，从而带来更稳定的优化轨迹，尤其是在强化学习场景中，on-policy 数据分布频繁变化的情况下。

11. Conclusion（11. 结论）

This report presented Megatron-Core MoE, an open-source stack for training large-scale Mixture-of-Experts models. MoE sparsity introduces two fundamental challenges: a parameter-compute mismatch that manifests as the Three Walls (Memory, Communication, and Compute Efficiency), and a dense-sparse mismatch that requires decoupled parallelism for attention and MoE layers. By combining integrated solutions across these challenges, Megatron-Core MoE enables efficient trillion-parameter-scale training.

本报告介绍了 Megatron-Core MoE，一个用于训练大规模混合专家模型的开源软件栈。混合专家稀疏性带来了两个根本性挑战：参数-计算失配，表现为三堵墙（内存、通信与计算效率）；以及稠密-稀疏失配，要求为注意力层与混合专家层解耦并行配置。通过结合针对这些挑战的综合解决方案，Megatron-Core MoE 使高效的万亿参数规模训练成为可能。

The key technical contributions include:

关键技术贡献包括：

Multi-Dimensional Parallelism and Parallel Folding. Expert Parallelism integrates seamlessly with tensor, pipeline, context, and data parallelism. MoE Parallel Folding decouples attention and MoE layer configurations, breaking the restrictive $\text{EP}\leq\text{DP}$ constraint and enabling flexible parallelism mapping tailored to model architecture and hardware topology.

多维并行与并行折叠。专家并行（Expert Parallelism，EP）与张量并行、流水线并行、上下文并行和数据并行无缝集成。混合专家并行折叠解耦了注意力层与混合专家层的配置，打破了限制性的 $\text{EP}\leq\text{DP}$ 约束，实现了针对模型架构与硬件拓扑量身定制的灵活并行映射。

Memory Optimization. Fine-grained activation recomputation, memory-efficient permutation, precision-aware optimizers with BF16 moments, and CPU activation offloading reduce memory footprint from 199.5 GB to under 80 GB per GPU for DeepSeek-V3, making trillion-parameter training feasible on current hardware.

内存优化。细粒度激活重计算、内存高效置换、采用 BF16 矩的精度感知优化器（precision-aware optimizer）以及 CPU 激活卸载将 DeepSeek-V3 每 GPU 的内存占用从 199.5 GB 降至 80 GB 以下，使万亿参数训练在当前硬件上成为可行。

Communication Optimization. High-performance token dispatchers (DeepEP and HybridEP), and communication-computation overlap transform all-to-all from a bottleneck into a background operation hidden behind expert computation.

通信优化。高性能 token 分发器（DeepEP 与混合专家并行）以及通信-计算重叠将全互联从瓶颈转变为隐藏在专家计算之后的后台操作。

Compute Efficiency. Grouped GEMM kernels batch expert computations for better hardware utilization, kernel fusion consolidates routing and permutation operations, CUDA Graphs reduce launch overhead, and sync-free execution eliminates host-device synchronization for dropless MoE.

计算效率。分组 GEMM 内核将专家计算打包以提高硬件利用率，内核融合整合路由与置换操作，CUDA 图减少启动开销，无同步执行消除了无丢弃混合专家（dropless MoE）的宿主机-设备同步。

FP8 Training. Full FP8 support with selective precision, protecting numerically sensitive components (router, embeddings, optimizer states) while aggressively quantizing bulk computation, provides benefits across all three walls simultaneously.

FP8 训练。完整的 FP8 支持配合选择性精度（selective precision），在积极量化批量计算的同时保护数值敏感组件（路由器、embedding、优化器状态），从而同时在三堵墙上带来收益。

Long-Context Training. Context Parallelism and Tensor Parallelism scaling maintain constant sub-sequence lengths per device, enabling training at 16K to 64K+ token sequences where attention computation dominates.

长上下文训练。上下文并行与张量并行扩展使每设备的子序列长度保持恒定，从而支持 16K 至 64K+ token 序列的训练，在这些序列长度下注意力计算占主导。

Production Features. Load balancing strategies and token dropping ensure stable training, distributed checkpointing enables parallelism-agnostic resharding, upcycling initializes MoE models from dense checkpoints, and multi-token prediction integration supports advanced training objectives.

生产特性。负载均衡策略与 token 丢弃（token dropping）确保训练稳定，分布式检查点支持与并行无关的重分片，模型升级转换（upcycling）从稠密检查点初始化混合专家模型，多 token 预测集成支持高级训练目标。

Reinforcement Learning Support. Megatron Core and Megatron-Bridge provide seamless integration with popular RL frameworks, while packed sequence support, dynamic context parallelism, and router replay address the unique challenges of RL post-training workloads.

强化学习支持。Megatron Core 与 Megatron-Bridge 为主流强化学习框架提供无缝集成，而打包序列支持、动态上下文并行与路由器重放则解决了强化学习后训练工作负载的独特挑战。

These optimizations enable Megatron-Core MoE to achieve strong training throughput: DeepSeek-V3 (685B parameters, 256 experts) trains at 1,233/1,048 TFLOPS per GPU on 256 GB300/GB200s and 368 TFLOPS per GPU on 1,024 H100s; Qwen3-235B achieves 974/919 TFLOPS on GB300/GB200 and 320 TFLOPS on H100. The GB300 and GB200 platforms deliver approximately 3 $\times$ higher token throughput compared to H100, demonstrating the framework’s ability to exploit next-generation hardware.

这些优化使 Megatron-Core MoE 取得了强劲的训练吞吐：DeepSeek-V3（685B 参数、256 个专家）在 256 台 GB300/GB200 上每 GPU 为 1,233/1,048 TFLOPS，在 1,024 台 H100 上为 368 TFLOPS/GPU；Qwen3-235B 在 GB300/GB200 上达到 974/919 TFLOPS，在 H100 上为 320 TFLOPS。GB300 与 GB200 平台的 token 吞吐约为 H100 的 3 $\times$，展示了该框架利用新一代硬件的能力。

By open-sourcing these capabilities, Megatron-Core MoE provides researchers and practitioners with production-grade tools for MoE experimentation and deployment at scale. The modular architecture enables rapid prototyping while the full optimization stack supports training from research prototypes to trillion-parameter production models.

通过开源这些能力，Megatron-Core MoE 为研究人员与实践者提供了用于大规模混合专家实验与部署的生产级工具。模块化架构支持快速原型设计，而完整的优化栈则支持从研究原型到万亿参数生产模型的训练。

Appendix A：Notation Reference（附录 A：符号参考）

Table 19 summarizes the notation used throughout this report.

表 19 汇总了本报告通篇使用的符号。

表： Table 19 : Notation and abbreviations used throughout this report.

表 19：本报告通篇使用的符号与缩写。

符号 说明
模型参数
$E$ MoE 层中的专家数量
$K$ Top-$k$ 路由宽度（每 token 激活的专家数；$K \ll E$）
$h$ 隐藏维度
$N$ 模型总参数量
$N_{\text{active}}$ 每 token 激活的参数量（随 $K$ 扩展）
$N_{\text{total}}$ 包含全部专家的总参数量（随 $E$ 扩展）
训练维度
$T$ 每 GPU 的本地 token 数
$B$ 批次大小（每批次的 token 数）
$s$ 序列长度
$L$ MoE 层数
并行
TP 张量并行度
PP 流水线并行度
CP 上下文并行度
DP 数据并行度
EP 专家并行度
ETP 专家张量并行度（MoE 专属）
EDP 专家数据并行度（MoE 专属）
VPP 虚拟流水线并行（交错流水线阶段）
批次配置
MBS 微批次大小
GBS 全局批次大小
GA 梯度累积步数
精度格式
BF16 BFloat16（16 位脑浮点）
FP8-BLK 采用分块缩放量化的 FP8（Hopper）
MXFP8 原生张量支持的微缩放 FP8（Blackwell）
指标与缩写
MFU 模型 FLOPs 利用率
GEMM 通用矩阵乘法
SDPA 缩放点积注意力
MLA 多潜注意力
MTP 多 token 预测

Appendix B：Detailed Benchmark Configurations（附录 B：详细基准配置）

This section lists the parallelism configurations and detailed settings for reproducing the performance numbers reported in Table 11 . Each configuration is identified by its system, GPU count, and precision format, and the corresponding hyper-parameter string summarizes the parallelism layout and batch configuration.

本节列出复现表 11 所报告性能数字所需的并行配置与详细设置。每个配置以其系统、GPU 数量和精度格式标识，对应的超参数字符串概括了并行布局与批次配置。

B.2. Key Optimizations（关键优化）

This section summarizes the configuration of the most performance-critical optimization features used in our benchmark runs. Although the full optimization space is larger, these features consistently have first-order impact on throughput and are configured differently across workloads and platforms.

本节汇总基准测试中对性能最关键的优化特性的配置。尽管完整的优化空间更大，但这些特性始终对吞吐有一阶影响，并在不同工作负载与平台上配置各异。

DeepSeek-V3

DeepSeek-V3

Dispatcher: HybridEP on GB300/GB200; DeepEP on H100.

分发器：GB300/GB200 上为 HybridEP；H100 上为 DeepEP。

Recompute: None on GB300; mlp on GB200; up_proj, mlp on H100.

重计算：GB300 上无；GB200 上为 mlp；H100 上为 up_proj、mlp。

1F1B overlap: ON for GB300 and H100; OFF for GB200.

1F1B 重叠：GB300 与 H100 上开启；GB200 上关闭。

CUDA Graphs: attn, moe_router, moe_preprocess on GB300/GB200; OFF on H100.

CUDA 图：GB300/GB200 上为 attn、moe_router、moe_preprocess；H100 上关闭。

These settings are chosen to balance memory headroom, communication overhead, and kernel efficiency for maximum sustained throughput on each platform.

选择这些设置是为了在各平台上平衡显存余量、通信开销与内核效率，以实现最大的持续吞吐。

Qwen3-235B

Qwen3-235B

Dispatcher: HybridEP on GB300/GB200; DeepEP on H100.

分发器：GB300/GB200 上为 HybridEP；H100 上为 DeepEP。

Recompute: moe_act, layernorm on GB200 and H100.

重计算：GB200 与 H100 上为 moe_act、layernorm。

1F1B overlap: OFF for GB300; ON for GB200 and H100.

1F1B 重叠：GB300 上关闭；GB200 与 H100 上开启。

CUDA Graphs: attn, moe_router, moe_preprocess .

CUDA 图：attn、moe_router、moe_preprocess。

The resulting configuration pattern reflects a throughput-first tuning strategy under model- and hardware-specific constraints.

由此形成的配置模式反映了在模型与硬件特定约束下以吞吐优先的调优策略。

B.3. Reproducibility（可复现性）

The benchmark numbers in Table 11 can be reproduced through two supported workflows. First, users can run end-to-end training with Megatron-Bridge 11 11 11 https://github.com/NVIDIA-NeMo/Megatron-Bridge/tree/main/scripts/performance , which provides a higher-level interface for model, parallelism, and optimization configuration. Second, users can launch training directly with Megatron-Core (MCore) using model-specific scripts in Megatron-MoE-ModelZoo 12 12 12 https://github.com/yanring/Megatron-MoE-ModelZoo/tree/main/best_practice/readme.md , which exposes the full set of low-level launch flags for performance tuning. In both workflows, start from Table 20 and adjust only a few cluster-specific runtime settings (for example, hostfile, environment modules, and job scheduler options).

表 11 中的基准数字可通过两种支持的工作流复现。第一，用户可以使用 Megatron-Bridge（https://github.com/NVIDIA-NeMo/Megatron-Bridge/tree/main/scripts/performance）运行端到端训练，它为模型、并行与优化配置提供了更高层的接口。第二，用户可以直接使用 Megatron-Core（MCore），通过 Megatron-MoE-ModelZoo（https://github.com/yanring/Megatron-MoE-ModelZoo/tree/main/best_practice/readme.md）中针对模型的脚本启动训练，它暴露了用于性能调优的全部底层启动参数。在两种工作流中，均从表 20 出发，仅调整少量集群特定的运行时设置（例如 hostfile、环境模块与作业调度器选项）。
